market comparison inconclusive while the supported prototype routes proceed.


3.27    Require the Java engine to preserve the decision target

The Java survey now distinguishes H2O-3 uplift forests from Tetrad’s graph-compatible slopes and
stability rankings. The selected source revisions precede the 7 October 2026 cutoff. H2O returns
treated/control response predictions and their difference; its selected ATE/ATT/ATC metrics
average predicted scores over retained rows. The selected training interface rejects weights and
built-in cross-validation. Tetrad’s minimum absolute effects rank magnitude and do not retain
benefit direction by themselves. Neither a ranking curve nor selection frequency establishes a
calibrated causal interval. [180, 181]
Exact authored examples show why these distinctions matter to the proposal. A confounded
population has observed response difference 0.36 despite zero causal effect; assumed value USD 100
and cost USD 20 turn an apparent USD 16 margin into USD -20. Scores with identical rankings
can have average predictions 0.25 and 0.50. Signed graph candidates -2 and -0.5 have minimum
magnitude 0.5, but treating that magnitude as beneficial changes hypothetical margins -220/-70
into USD 30. These are transparent constructions, not measured package or bank results. [182]
The public prototype should first reconcile a documented-versus-literal KL split expression, missing-
support and root-only branches, category mappings, and complete engine/MOJO roundtrips. The
equal-probability source expression gives -1 where the documented KL node value is zero; no
native failure is asserted. A proposed Java worker must return typed predictions, graph candidates,
rankings, numerical failures and qualified uncertainty separately through the library, CLI and
MCP. Complementary software search, full distribution rights and held-out validation remain
open; the source review supports a concrete experiment, not runtime admission. [180, 181, 182]


3.28    Use literature graphs to propose testable interventions

Tong and colleagues offer a concrete design for combining literature extraction, graph links and
hypothesis generation. Their July 2024 article reports 43,312 psychology papers and expert ratings
of generated well-being hypotheses. Those ratings concern novelty and usefulness; they do not
experimentally validate the proposed mechanisms. Of 238 pairs labelled causal by the model, 156
received matching human causal labels, while 207 were recognized as relationships of some kind.
The distinction matters when translating a business question into a causal diagram. [183]
The proposal should evaluate source-supported extraction, operational question translation, blind
hypothesis quality and intervention identification separately. A graph-similarity construction has
Jaccard score one with zero causal effect: at assumed value USD 100 and cost USD 20, treating
similarity as success would suggest USD 80 while the actual stipulated incremental margin is USD
-20. These are authored examples, not bank results. The source also contains printed ANOVA
quantities that do not reconcile under the ordinary stated model; raw analysis and the revised
supplement remain necessary before inheriting those inferential claims. [184, 183]
A public prototype can compare retrieval-only, model-only, graph-assisted and human-assisted
pipelines with held-out document and mechanism families. It should retain unsupported claims,

                                                 19
Causal banking: product proposal                                                  23 October 2026


reviewer disagreement, refusals and full model/expert costs. Library, CLI and MCP outputs
should distinguish an extracted claim, a hypothesis, a reviewed assumption and an identified
query. Code adoption, exact models, graph conventions and full rights remain open. [183, 184]


3.29    Preserve the meaning of repeated servicing policies

The gfoRmula review recommends retaining its pinned original R implementation as a reference
candidate for longitudinal simulation. The inspected source distinguishes terminal outcomes from
survival risks, orders evolving covariates before interventions, and resamples whole people with
their histories. It declares version 1.1.2 but leaves the release date as TBD. A future worker
must preserve the intervention clock, competing-event target, range clipping and requested versus
successful bootstrap counts. Native execution, complete dependency rights and qualification for
shared bank or calendar shocks remain open.[185]
An exact generated two-period example gives beneficial-outcome probabilities of 0.13 under never
acting, 0.60 under always acting and 0.425 under a specified dynamic policy. Replacing individual
histories with conditional means changes the dynamic calculation to 0.365. At an assumed USD
100 per additional beneficial result and a fixed incremental USD 20 cost per eligible account, the
proper dynamic-policy margin is USD 9.50; the shortcut gives USD 3.50. A separate competing-
event example gives risks 0.3096 and 0.36 in different event worlds, a USD 50.40 difference at an
assumed USD 1,000 loss. These are reproducible design references, not package benchmark results
or bank returns.[186]
The proposed route is to compare a frozen original R worker with these exact public mechanisms
before considering a port. The comparison must expose clipping, history conventions, failed fits,
numerical error and uncertainty under the declared sampling unit. Library, CLI and MCP should
return the same typed target and failure information. This completes a bounded proposal choice
while leaving implementation and broader software coverage open.[185, 186]


3.30    Distinguish explaining a channel from changing it

Stata and Mplus provide documented mediation workflows that our proposed service would
need to preserve or compare. Stata’s selected one-mediator interface distinguishes natural and
controlled effects, interactions and output scales; its two-mediator feature belongs separately to
StataNow. The inspected Mplus chapter distinguishes conventional coefficient products, including
latent-response scales, from counterfactual effects. These are documented capabilities; our current
comparison has exact reference examples and proposed interfaces, without a qualified vendor
adapter.[187, 188, 189, 190, 191]
An exact interaction example decomposes a total effect of 15 as either 6+9 or 9+6, depending on
the natural-effect definition. A separate pair of generated worlds has identical observed data and
randomized treatment but opposite channel decompositions because one world contains unobserved
mediator-outcome confounding. The original treatment gives an assumed margin of USD 80;
directly changing the mediator gives USD -20 under the stipulated law. Channel attribution
therefore cannot be treated automatically as the return from a channel intervention. Averaging
over customers versus evaluating at mean covariates also changes an exact example’s assumed
margin from USD 5 to USD -5. A residual-correlation sensitivity range remains a scenario range
rather than a confidence interval.[192]
Commercial coverage remains inconclusive because SAS, IBM/AMOS and PROCESS still need
matched technical originals. The PROCESS author page identifies it as a separate extension
for SPSS, SAS and R; that does not establish native host capability or distribution rights. The
proposal now specifies typed mediation targets, uncertainty, sensitivity, failure and refusal across


                                                20
Causal banking: product proposal                                                     23 October 2026


library, CLI and MCP, with exact public mechanisms for the first qualification experiment. Full
version, license, price, sensitivity coverage and native parity reviews remain open.[193]


3.31    Preserve the meaning of native heterogeneous effects

A fast causal forest is useful only if its result answers the requested action and population question.
The inspected grf, bcf, XBCF and flexBCF versions offer different moment, prior and posterior
designs. The proposed initial route is a qualified process adapter preserving each native model’s
assumptions, weights, draw identities and persistence. A direct port would transfer responsibility
for all of those numerical and statistical choices. The current review grants no runtime admission.
[194, 195, 196, 197]
Exact project examples show why these distinctions affect a decision. A continuous-treatment
covariance slope can be zero while a finite action contrast is one. For two equally represented
groups, an effect of USD 2 becomes USD 5.20 if likelihood precision is mistakenly reused as
population weighting: at USD 3 cost, the apparent margin changes from USD -1 to USD 2.20.
Restoring posterior treatment scales draw by draw gives a mean of USD 22.50 in another example;
omitting a scale factor gives USD 15. At USD 20 cost the corresponding margins are USD 2.50
and USD -5. Common posterior draws must also be retained when aggregating customers. These
are hypothetical, reproduced examples, not measured package failures or banking returns. [198]
The genuine inspected BCF preprint supports a public evaluation and cautions against blanket
acceptance. Its retrospective ACIC comparison reports 82% coverage for BCF intervals labeled
nominal 95%; the paper does not claim double robustness or a universal coverage theorem.
Historical performance does not rank today’s source versions. The next prototype should measure
matched target error, coverage, width, failures and decision consequences under known public
mechanisms before expensive institutional trials. [199]
Static XBCF wrapper inconsistencies and flexBCF row/cohort conventions require focused tests.
The broader C++ census, additional method originals, dependency rights and native parity remain
unresolved. This is a bounded, inconclusive software-coverage result with concrete integration
choices and tests; it does not close the complete software repair. [196, 197]


3.32    Evaluate paired reasoning errors and the full request population

CausalityCheck provides a four-by-four task and robustness design for testing textual causal
reasoning. Its genuine July 2026 ACL paper reports 1,200 seed questions, 43,200 generated
instances, 10,800 selected and 8,920 retained after filtering. The framework is useful for a
prototype evaluation, but its textual labels and accuracy do not establish identified banking effects.
Some reported annotation-agreement values also fall below the paper’s broad excellent-agreement
description. [200]
A difference between answerability and process-judgment accuracy is a net difference of two
discordance rates. It does not identify the frequency of correct answers supported by wrong
reasoning. In an authored matched-population example, marginals of 82.27% and 59.46% permit
that joint error rate to range from 22.81% to 40.54%. A negative difference of -5.12 points can
coexist with a rate as high as 23.2%. The paper’s actual task populations differ, so these are
illustrations of the required measurement design, not empirical bounds on its models. [201]
Filtering also changes the population. If 50 of 100 requests are retained and 45 are correct, selected
accuracy is 90%, while full-population accuracy can range from 45% to 95%. Under explicitly
assumed USD 10 gain, USD 30 error loss and USD 1 action cost, selected accuracy suggests USD
5 margin; the full-population range is USD -13 to USD 7. A real refusal policy requires its own
costs and workload accounting. These are exact hypothetical witnesses, not measured returns.

                                                  21
Causal banking: product proposal                                                    23 October 2026


[201]
The proposed public evaluation uses common seed identities, both discordance directions, known
causal mechanisms, blind adjudication, inclusion/refusal records and fixed aggregation weights.
Entity renaming is a stress test whose score changes can have several explanations. Pinned
code/data, raw paired judgments, licenses and independent reproduction remain open; no live
model or upstream package has been executed. [200]


3.33    Preserve the customer population when reusing a causal forest

The grf R package and its native core are one implementation lineage. The original pinned R
package remains a reference candidate for an initial binary-action, independent-customer prototype.
Reusing it would preserve the nuisance-fitting and average-effect wrapper as well as the C++
forest. Integration, dependency rights, calibration, persistence and full workflow costs still require
qualification. The proposed process boundary does not itself settle distribution rights. [202]
The wrapper’s doubly robust score reconstructs two treatment-specific outcome regressions from
the supplied marginal outcome mean, propensity and conditional effect. An exact hypothetical law
shows why those inputs need precise provenance: with true propensity one quarter and effect two,
supplying the correct marginal mean and effect but a propensity of one half yields an expected
score of 1.5. Correct propensity or both correctly reconstructed outcome regressions restores two
in this example. This is an algebraic qualification fixture, not an observed fitted-model failure or
a coverage claim. [203]
An overlap-weighted effect also changes the business population. In two equally represented groups
with effects USD -4 and USD 12 and propensities one half and one tenth, the all-customer effect is
USD 4 but the overlap effect is USD 4/17. At an assumed offer cost of USD 2, the corresponding
margins are USD 2 and approximately USD -1.76. The second margin cannot be substituted
for the first without changing the decision. A prototype should retain target identity, nuisance
provenance, actual estimator, warnings and all failed requests, and compare accepted outputs
before claiming an economic advantage. [202, 203]


3.34    Treat document extraction as a proposed causal structure

The ACE study supplies three reported routes from failure documents to bowtie diagrams: retrieval
from digital tables, OCR from table images, and vision extraction followed by a second language
model. Selected structured-table results are substantially stronger than narrative results. The
original evaluates three tabular components and dynamic-seal narratives, with expert reference
diagrams that can include implicit information absent from the source. A banking prototype
should distinguish explicit source evidence, expert-added hypotheses, missing roles and model
inference before asking an intervention question. [204]
High aggregate graph accuracy can hide a consequential omission. An authored 100-edge chain
with one missing bridge has edge F1 of 198/199, about 0.995, yet loses the entire initiating-event-
to-loss path. A second exact example gives a zero first-order sensitivity index but a total-order
index of one: a small main effect alone does not show that context pipelines are interchangeable.
Both examples motivate held-out document tests, critical-role/path metrics, paired comparisons
and preservation of every invalid output. They are mathematical witnesses, not reproduced ACE
performance. [205]
A common graph topology also does not price a control. With the same unsigned action-to-loss
arrow, two stipulated mechanisms give intervention effects of opposite sign. At assumed loss USD
10 and action cost USD 2, their margins are USD -12 and USD 8. The next public prototype
should retain source spans, clocked roles, mechanism assumptions, refusal states and full review

                                                 22
Causal banking: product proposal                                                   23 October 2026


costs. ACE’s proposed agentic correction and knowledge-graph extensions remain future work;
neither the paper nor this review establishes an implemented banking integration. [204, 205]


3.35    Preserve missingness assumptions when extending a pilot

A profitable pilot need not justify a rollout to a different customer mix. The commercial comparison
now inspects ten complete official originals for Stata MI, SAS/STAT 14.1 MI/MIANALYZE and
IBM SPSS Missing Values 31.0.2. These systems document substantive workflows that a new
proposal must match: imputation identity and variable roles, appropriate conditional models,
diagnostics, completed-data estimates and uncertainty combination. Exact licensed builds and
broader vendor coverage remain unresolved. [206, 207, 208, 209]
The comparison exposes important requirements for integration. Stata’s sample-change and
estimation-failure guards must survive an automatic wrapper. IBM distinguishes parameter-only
pooling from pooling with uncertainty and does not equate accepting analysis weights with handling
complex sampling. SAS distinguishes generation from combination and documents assumption-
sensitive inference. A stable imputation trace does not establish compatible conditional models,
and a missingness sensitivity analysis does not discover the missing outcomes. The proposed
interfaces need to preserve these distinctions. [210, 211, 212, 213, 214, 215]
Exact authored examples show the monetary consequence. With stratum effects USD 10 and
USD -10, moving from an 80/20 pilot mix to a 20/80 target mix changes the effect from 6 to -6.
At assumed action cost USD 2, margins change from USD 4 to USD -8. A separate example has
identical observed records under two missing-outcome laws but complete-population effects 0 and
10, giving margins USD -4 and USD 6 at assumed cost USD 4. Neither more imputation draws nor
a weight column resolves absent identifying information. These are reproducible counterexamples,
not measured bank returns. [216]
The first investment should therefore support matched public or generated tests before expensive
bank trials. Compare declared populations and missingness laws, covariance and failure handling,
sensitivity, coverage, interval width and complete workflow cost. A licensed isolated worker is
a proposed reuse route; native adapters, engine redistribution rights and prices have not been
qualified. The contribution under consideration is a consistent causal and uncertainty contract
across library, CLI and MCP use.


3.36    Match software outputs to the servicing decision

The Julia comparison distinguishes CausalELM 0.8.0, CausalForest 0.1.5, TMLE 0.20.5 and
CausalTables 1.3.4 at selected revisions preceding the literature cutoff. Their inspected source
supports different roles: fitted effect contrasts, tree predictions, targeted mean estimation and
supplied-model reference generation. These roles need distinct adoption decisions. [217, 218, 219,
220]
An exact public example gives a population effect of USD 4 but a residual regression coefficient
of 4/17 dollars. At cost USD 2, the population margin is USD 2 while the coefficient-based
calculation is negative. Another example with missingness depending only on observed risk
changes the complete-case average to -12/5 dollars. Neither a method name nor a more accurate
treatment propensity model settles the required population. The selected CausalELM standard-
error expression also has squared outcome units; converting dollars to cents scales it by 10,000
instead of 100. These are exact authored and source-level findings, not native runtime results.
[221]
The proposal should prioritize a bounded TMLE worker design and CausalTables public reference
generation while preserving influence scales, missingness targets, unit identities and the provenance

                                                 23
Causal banking: product proposal                                                  23 October 2026


of supplied graphs. CausalELM’s selected uncertainty path needs repair and fresh qualification;
the inspected CausalForest prediction paths do not supply a calibrated variance object. Additional
Rust candidates were found in bounded official metadata searches, but their technical originals
and historical versions remain unread. [222]


3.37    Preserve the target when adopting panel software

The selected gsynth 1.4.0 is a compatibility wrapper around fect; the reviewed engine candidate is
fect 2.4.5. The complete wrapper declares a documented weight argument but never forwards it.
A stored request can therefore say weights were requested without establishing that the engine
used them. The wrapper also remaps estimator and inference choices and supplies defaults that
differ from direct fect use. This is a pinned source finding; no native execution or general package
failure claim is made. [223, 224]
Exact public examples show the stakes. Effects of USD -6 and USD 10 average USD 2, but weights
9:1 give USD -4.4. At assumed cost USD 1, the corresponding margins are USD 1 and USD -5.4.
A separate example gives a treated-period average of USD 6 and an equal-unit average of USD
2, with margins USD 2 and USD -2 at cost USD 4. A deterministic factor example preserves
the complete observed panel while allowing effects USD 6 or USD 2 because a factor direction is
absent before treatment. Adding pre-period rows need not add that information. These examples
are outside full-rank identification and are not native estimator failures or observed bank returns.
[225]
The proposal keeps the wrapper as a reference and identifies a direct fect worker as a future
candidate. Admission requires explicit target and weight roles, method and dependency pins,
retained-sample and donor records, rank and convergence diagnostics, and an uncertainty contract
that counts failed fits and bootstrap draws. Source inspection also finds restrictions on treatment
reversals, distinct fitting/aggregation weight columns and clustered jackknife. Complete license-
template and dependency review, native parity and declared-law calibration remain prototype
obligations. Public panels and known-law examples can establish the value of that work before
expensive bank experiments.


3.38    Test causal calculation support against an audited reference

Shrestha, Malberg and Groh’s causal program of thoughts generates DoWhy/Python code, executes
it and returns the result to the model. On its formal synthetic benchmark, GPT-4o’s reported
overall accuracy rises from 82.0% to 90.8%, an 8.8 percentage-point difference; a causal tree
of thoughts reaches 86.4%. The commonsensical subset has different scores: 84.3%, 92.5% and
87.5%, respectively. These results motivate a public prototype of calculation support. They do
not establish faithful business translation, graph identification or effects from authentic banking
observations. The study excludes backdoor-adjustment questions and generates artificial data
from facts supplied in the question.[226, 227]
The reference answers need scrutiny too. Sample query counts sum to 999 while rung counts
sum to 1,000; an error-category table totals 60 although the prose describes 50 cases. Literal
evaluation of two different expressions in an appendix mediation example gives -0.112 and 0.0245,
with the latter printed as 0.0173. Outcome coding and the intended target also need reconciliation.
These are documented inconsistencies, not a declaration of corrected benchmark truth. Raw
question-level records and code are needed before replication or reuse.[226, 227]
For a payment-holiday decision, the assistant must preserve the offer, population, information
available before action, loss horizon and accounting target. A correct calculation for customers
who accept an offer can answer a different question from the effect of making it. A public prototype


                                                24
Causal banking: product proposal                                                  23 October 2026


should compare verbal answers, exact formula checks, constrained library calls and generated
programs on the same known-law cases. Score translation, identification, numerical accuracy,
justified refusal and full workflow cost separately. Freeze disputed references before evaluation,
retain failures and test renamed or contradictory cases without leaking paraphrases across splits.
CLI and MCP requests should carry the target, source evidence and unresolved assumptions;
successful execution alone cannot authorize a causal conclusion. This supplies a testable route
before costly bank studies, without assuming a qualified implementation or measured return.


3.39    Saved results support a prototype, with specific reuse repairs

The paper-linked repository now supplies authenticated prompts, code and three RQ3 result
tables. All twenty-four reported subset accuracy averages reproduce against the saved labels
on the same 1,000 question identifiers. The sample has 138 treatment-on-the-treated questions,
resolving the printed count of 137. In the commonsensical subset, the program corrects 141
baseline errors but loses 59 previously correct answers. These paired records support a more
informative public comparison; they are not a fresh model trial or independent validation of the
reference labels.[228, 229]
The appendix mediation example maps to question 2883. Its unrounded probabilities yield
about -0.112176, agreeing with its saved numerical target. The death/survival wording and
formal notation remain unresolved. Empty self-consistency histories and missing error-category
annotations prevent reconciliation of the other appendix counts. These findings qualify the earlier
paper-only account of missing records.[229]
Unchanged execution is unsuitable: the selected self-consistency caller and helper have incompatible
arguments, client plumbing is incomplete, and the README’s pip instruction points to a platform-
specific Conda export. The illustrative program prompt also omits a common cause from its
graph. Under its intended independent-draw generator, the treatment effect is zero while the
observational difference is 6/793. A successful numerical call could therefore answer the wrong
causal question.[228, 229]
Retain the material as a research reference, repair the runner and environment in a separate
prototype, and require agreement between the mechanism, graph and estimand. Audit inherited-
data and dependency rights beyond the root MIT license. Compare a constrained library and an
exact formula checker with generated code before paying for bank experiments. The saved-score
audit justifies a concrete next test; it does not establish a qualified product or measured economic
benefit.


3.40    Hidden-state tools need a causal and information contract

The commercial Bayesian/state comparison now distinguishes five supported vendor families
from five families still needing matched originals. Stata documents linear-state estimation, sep-
arate filtered and smoothed predictions, explicit Bayesian priors and simulation diagnostics.
GeNIe/SMILE supplies fixed-structure DBN learning and temporal representations; Netica sup-
plies parameter-learning alternatives; BayesiaLab distinguishes observation from intervention;
Mplus supplies a Bayesian dynamic-SEM route. These are documented strengths. Exact builds,
deployment rights, prices and native comparisons remain unresolved. The failed SAS, EViews and
HUGIN acquisitions are not absence findings. [230, 231, 232, 233, 234, 235, 73, 236, 237, 238]
The banking examples explain why an adapter needs more than an inference call. Two specified
mechanisms give identical observed action/default data but imply default reductions of zero and
0.6. At an assumed USD 1,000 loss and USD 100 action cost, their margins are USD -100 and USD
500. Posterior model odds remain prior odds in this example; priors of 0.1 and 0.9 on the causal


                                                25
Causal banking: product proposal                                                    23 October 2026


mechanism give margins of USD -40 and USD 440. This is a transparent sensitivity calculation,
not evidence that either mechanism describes bank customers. [239]
A separate exact example produces a filtered distress probability of 0.75 and a later smoothed
probability of 0.5. With the stipulated causal benefit and cost, the respective margins are
USD 15 and USD -10. The future observation was not available when the action was chosen.
Stata’s documented prediction RMSE also excludes parameter-estimation error, and its Bayesian
simulation exports compress repeated states into frequency-weighted rows. Dropping those
frequencies changes the example posterior mean from 0.18 to 0.5. A usable common service must
preserve both the information clock and the meaning of uncertainty. [231, 232, 239]
The proposed route is a versioned licensed worker with explicit model and intervention assumptions,
observation availability, parameter and state mappings, chain metadata, diagnostics and refusal
conditions. Exact public laws can test these semantics before costly bank trials. They can also
expose unobservable state contrasts and near-threshold decisions sensitive to numerical error. The
present evidence establishes a proposal and reproducible reference cases; it establishes neither a
working vendor adapter nor measured commercial value. [239]


3.41    Instruments and credit thresholds require different decision targets

The R comparison now distinguishes six pinned packages: AER and ivreg for separate regression
interfaces; ivmodel for model-specific weak-instrument and sensitivity procedures; rdrobust for
continuity-based threshold inference; rddensity for density diagnostics; and rdlocrand for local
randomization. The selected rdrobust version is 4.0.0: the 4.1.1 CRAN publication timestamp
falls after the exact client cutoff despite its 7 October package date. [240, 241, 242, 243, 244, 245]
An exact hypothetical credit example gives a USD 150 loss reduction among compliers and USD
70 across the full population. With a USD 100 treatment cost, these imply margins of USD 50
and USD -30 respectively. A separate encouragement policy has margin USD 10 after incremental
take-up and encouragement costs. The complier effect cannot be substituted for the effect of
treating everyone. Another exact example shows that two continuous loss models agree at all
observed integer scores while implying threshold effects of USD -20 and zero. A declared slope
bound yields a net-margin set of USD -10 to USD 30 rather than a uniformly profitable action.
These are authored laws, not measured bank returns. [246]
The proposed interfaces must preserve disconnected and unbounded confidence sets, distinguish an
empty finite search grid from a globally empty set, and separate density diagnostics from evidence
for the causal design. Inspection of ivmodel’s pinned CLR source found numerical-error branches
that substitute zero; native integration needs an explicit way to detect or repair those paths before
treating their outputs as valid inference. The five reuse dispositions are reference-only; no R
adapter or package benchmark has been executed. [242, 246]
Original R workers are preferred candidates for a bounded prototype because porting adds
numerical and maintenance obligations. The actual distribution still needs dependency and rights
review: inspected package-level license texts, including ivmodel’s MIT alternative, do not clear
every dependency or packaging choice. Public/generated conformance cases and prospectively
specified calibration studies can address these questions before a costly bank experiment. [247,
248, 242]


4      What domain-knowledge assistance can justify

Two clinical studies support testing an assistant that proposes variables and retrieves evidence for
causal graphs. They do not establish reliable autonomous graph construction. In a fifteen-output


                                                 26
Causal banking: product proposal                                                     23 October 2026


statin scenario, graphs were usually acyclic but sources were sometimes missing or nonexistent. A
separate chronic-pain preprint reports F1 of 0.745 for combined graph retrieval and observational
discovery, versus 0.714 for ordinary retrieval and 0.737 for graph retrieval alone. The expert
reference contains cycles and was built from the same literature supplied to retrieval. These are
useful, qualified reconstruction results; they are not independent intervention validation.[249, 250]
An exact proposal example gives two Gaussian models with identical observed records but different
servicing effects. At stipulated USD100 per loss unit and USD20 incremental cost, their modeled
margins are USD30 and USD-20. More confident graph prose cannot resolve the difference. A
public prototype should test source accuracy, direct versus indirect relations, conflicting evidence,
abstention and valid identification before measuring decision value. Compare ordinary and graph
retrieval at matched evidence budgets and include graph construction and human review costs.[251]
The proposed adoption decision is to use typed evidence and relation interfaces, retain uncertain
alternatives and require a matched public benefit test before porting a retrieval stack or funding
private bank experiments. No live model, native package or bank workflow was executed in this
review.


5    What commercial observation infrastructure can support

Customer matching, feature retrieval and storage history solve different parts of the evidence prob-
lem. AWS Entity Resolution distinguishes matching rules, group confidence and, for incremental
ML, record confidence. IBM QualityStage documents tunable linkage and specification review.
Databricks provides historical feature joins, but documents different lookback behavior for train-
ing/batch and online inference. Snowflake provides retained storage states with edition, schema
and transaction-boundary limits. These are conditional integration candidates; the proposal does
not yet have qualified native adapters. Exact cutoff-era versions, commercial terms and four other
candidate families remain unresolved. [252, 253, 254, 255, 256, 257]
The commercial opportunity is to preserve the meaning needed for a causal decision across these
systems. A historical event timestamp does not establish when information became usable. A
match identifier does not independently verify customer ownership. In an exact hypothetical
example, two possible complete linkage maps give contrasts +4 and -4. Giving the first map
probability 0.8 yields an averaged contrast 2.4. With USD 10 per outcome unit and USD 30 offer
cost, accepting the first map gives USD 10 margin, while the mixture gives USD -6. The mixture
probability and costs are stipulated, not vendor calibration or measured banking returns. Another
example changes the population effect from 4 to 5.2 merely by selecting which records remain
linked. [258]
The next justified test uses generated customer histories with hidden identity truth, late arrivals,
false merges, excluded records and known treatment effects. It should compare matched baselines
and report linkage errors, future-information violations, effect distortion and decision loss. Library,
CLI and MCP must expose the same provenance and refusals. This public route can establish
prototype merit before paying for commercial qualification or an experiment with private bank
data.


6    What sensitivity and IV engines must preserve

A branch-offer estimate can look attractive because a noisy pretrend has been treated as known.
A causal proposal must instead connect the substantive trend restriction, the joint sampling
uncertainty and the offer’s monetary target. HonestDiD supplies a valuable R reference for
sensitivity to departures from parallel trends, supported by Rambachan and Roth’s identification

                                                  27
Causal banking: product proposal                                                     23 October 2026


and inference arguments. The proposed reuse choice is to retain that reference and consider an
isolated R worker after qualification. It does not yet admit a native engine. The selected repository
revision declares version 0.2.8; equality to a particular distributed release, solver behavior and
transitive dependency rights remain unqualified. A new port would add statistical and numerical
parity obligations without a demonstrated benefit [259, 260].
The economic distinction is concrete. In a three-period example, normalize the untreated group
difference in the reference period to zero and call its earlier value x. Let the postperiod contrast
be y = τ + δ1 , where τ is the target offer effect. Bounding the next untreated first difference by
a multiplier m gives effects in [y − m|x|, y + m|x|]. Bounding the change in that first difference
by an absolute amount M instead gives [y + x − M, y + x + M ]. At x = −2, y = 4, setting the
respective tuning values to zero gives effects 4 and 2: a flat differential trend and continuation of
a linear differential trend are different assumptions. The multiplier is dimensionless, while the
smoothness bound has outcome units on a specified calendar [259, 261].
An authored known-Gaussian experiment then illustrates the value of retaining covariance. With
estimated pretrend 0, postperiod contrast 4, multiplier 1, variances 1 and 4 and covariance
1.5, projecting a joint 95% confidence ellipse through the relative-magnitude restriction gives
approximately [−2.9233, 10.9233]. A stipulated offer cost of USD 1 per exposure changes this to
net bounds of approximately [−3.9233, 9.9233]. The corresponding stipulated population example
has effect 4 and net value 3, but a confidently positive investment conclusion does not follow from
this interval. These are reproducible mathematical examples under a declared law, not measured
bank returns or an execution of HonestDiD’s optimized procedures [261].
An adapter must retain more than endpoints. The inspected R paths differ in default methods,
tuning grids and forwarding of options. Selected paths reduce accepted grid nodes to their
minimum and maximum; that hull can conceal gaps or unresolved search extent. Some inspected
population-bound failure branches can return the unadjusted target as both endpoints. These
source paths are not observed runtime failures, but they make solver status, grid membership,
warnings and the executed restriction necessary parts of the interface. Conversion from event-study
engines must preserve coefficient ordering, reference removal, target aggregation and full covariance
[260, 262].
Instrumental-variable engines require a parallel discipline. Separate full ranks of the action design
and the instrument matrix do not establish a full-rank projected design or a valid exclusion
restriction. An exact four-row witness has projected Gram 4ε2 and IV ratio 2 + 1/(100ε): a
stipulated small direct instrument effect changes the fitted ratio from 3 to 12 as relevance
shrinks. This motivates generated comparisons that vary instrument strength and exclusion
separately. The inspected Python paths also differ in weighting, column removal and covariance
defaults; heteroskedastic-robust covariance does not automatically address calendar dependence
[263, 264, 265].
Diagnostic names must preserve their actual null and reference distribution. A selected first-stage
helper can display a Wald quadratic divided by its restriction count while returning a chi-square
p-value. For quadratic 8 and two restrictions the displayed value is 4; chi-square-two and an F
distribution with 2 and 20 degrees of freedom give p-values about 0.0183 and 0.0346. Likewise,
an inspected Anderson–Rubin overidentification property is not a coefficient-null confidence-set
inversion. The library, CLI and MCP should therefore retain the restriction, distribution, degrees
of freedom, covariance and numerical status instead of unifying similarly named fields into a
generic interval [264, 266, 265].
The next investment is a bounded public prototype: frozen model conversions, known-law panels,
prespecified restrictions, malformed-input and solver-status cases, and matched decision compar-
isons that retain failures in the denominator. Its full cost includes fitting, covariance construction,
repeated inversions, environment support and investigation of refused results. Python regression


                                                  28
Causal banking: product proposal                                                    23 October 2026


discontinuity and CausalPy IV still require their own primary review. These specific remaining
tasks can establish whether native integration merits a later costly bank experiment.


7    Preserve evidence and the customer population

A causal assistant needs to check what the source supports and whose effect is being estimated. A
correct quotation can describe an association, a different customer population or an intervention
whose identifying assumptions do not hold for the proposed decision. Wang and colleagues’
factuality survey helps organize retrieval, attribution, human review and model judging. Its
surveyed finance language tasks do not establish causal validity, and its accounts of other studies
do not replace independent inspection of those originals. [267]
The proposal should retain evidence spans and their population/time scope, separate supported
premises from unverified causal assumptions, and expose conflicting or insufficient evidence.
Structured retrieval can help map entities and policies, but a knowledge-graph relationship is not
automatically a causal edge. Several agents agreeing on the same retrieved premise do not create
independent evidence. Compare these workflows against simpler retrieval and human-assisted
baselines with matched documents, review time and budgets. Evaluate citation support, causal
identification/refusal, target preservation and decision loss separately. These are proposed public
prototype studies, with actual reliability and cost still to be measured. [267]
Vilfort’s weighted-estimands paper addresses another failure route: different reasonable customer
mixtures can imply different effects even when group effects are valid. In an authored illustration,
stipulated group effects of USD 6 and USD -2 per customer give contribution USD 4 under weights
3/4 and 1/4, but contribution USD 0 under weights 1/4 and 3/4. A stipulated USD 1 cost changes
the respective net values to USD 3 and USD -1. Allowing a fraction epsilon of baseline weight
to move to any group gives a worst-case net value of 3-6 epsilon; strictly positive value requires
epsilon below 1/2. This illustrates target sensitivity, with no observed bank effects or unknown
population weights inferred from the example. [268, 269]
Sampling uncertainty adds a separate requirement. The paper’s exact model uses normally
distributed group-effect estimates, known positive-definite joint covariance and fixed known weight
classes. Its guarantees distinguish coverage for each fixed alternative target from simultaneous
coverage of the entire target set. At alpha=beta=0.05, the lower guarantees are 90% for each fixed
target and 85% for the simultaneous two-sided event. A possible allocation for a 95% simultaneous
guarantee is alpha=0.0125 and beta=0.025, under the exact assumptions. A result-selected
customer mix needs the appropriate protection; the words “95% confidence” alone cannot specify
it. [268, 269]
The feasible extensions require covariance/weight consistency and conditions on estimated classes.
Boundary targets need a containment argument; the paper’s positive-slack theorem cannot be
justified merely by unchanged rounded results at zero slack. The proposed library, CLI and
MCP output should carry the target, full covariance, class, confidence event, error budgets and
numerical status. No native robust-interval solver or adapter has been qualified here. Public
known-law effects and independent evidence corpora can test these requirements before expensive
bank experiments. Weight robustness still presupposes valid group effects and does not repair
confounding. [268]

An exact authored example uses three units with stipulated effect 6 and one with effect -2, giving

9    Preserve the intervention before investing in an answer

A recent survey of contextual causality draws a useful boundary for the proposed assistant.
Extracting a causal sentence, changing a story and answering a counterfactual question about
that story differ from identifying the effect of changing a bank action. The selected September
2026 survey explicitly makes this distinction. Its benchmark review and call for extrinsic decision
evaluation help design a prototype; they do not independently qualify every cited method or
establish monetary benefit from financial-text accuracy. [278]
An exact authored example shows why the distinction affects investment. Let an unobserved
account state be zero or one with equal probability, and let natural contact equal that state. In
one compatible law, gross response is USD 100 times contact; in another, it is USD 100 times the
latent state. Both produce the same observed contact/response pairs. At USD 10 per contact,
all-contact versus no-contact incremental net is USD 90 in the first law and USD -10 in the second.
Rephrasing the observations cannot resolve this ambiguity. A justified design or restriction must
supply the missing causal information; merely measuring the latent state would still leave no
within-state contact variation under this natural rule. These are stipulated laws and values, not
bank observations or model performance. [279]
The adjacent multimodal literature supplies another qualification. A selected 2024 original studies
conflicting textual and visual answers and proposes dynamic contrastive decoding. Its reported
LLaVA-34b gains in two Table 4 accuracy cells are 2.36 and 2.12 percentage points. An equal-


                                                30
Causal banking: product proposal                                                   23 October 2026


score cell, a cross-table baseline conflict and four inconsistent Table 5 subtractions limit broader
conclusions. Those contradictions remain explicit. Answer agreement can be jointly wrong;
disagreement does not identify hidden knowledge conflict or causal validity. The repository, code,
data and license remain unqualified. Public or generated chart/table/narrative trials can compare
correctness, conflict handling, refusal and total cost before a costly live bank study. [280, 279]
Software reuse must preserve the same discipline. The pinned linearmodels IV source supports a
reasoned reference choice with sample, weight, covariance and diagnostic contracts. Its NCSA
terms and build requirements are inspected, but native admission and transitive rights are not
established. Tetrad’s inspected two-stage class instead targets supplied pure latent measurement
models, including marker normalization. Source-level axis, inverse and local covariance concerns
require caller/backend reproduction before any executed failure is claimed. It does not establish
generic Java IV/RD coverage. [281, 282]
The lmtp reference makes policy conversion economically explicit. With two servicing contacts
and a declared known law, natural, risk-style shift and odds-tilt net values are USD 7.36, USD
18.24 and USD 107/9. The same parameter value of two represents different probability maps and
different targets. The inspected AGPL license, cluster-field naming, effective prediction bounds
and delegated variance semantics remain separate integration questions. No package execution or
bank return is asserted. These findings justify specific known-law policy/fold/contrast tests rather
than general adoption. [283, 284]

11     Preserve longitudinal regimes and the requested decision pop-
       ulation

The selected ltmle source is a useful R reference for binary longitudinal treatment and censoring
histories, treatment-specific means, and a working marginal structural model. Its interface supports
a single regime or a pair; the working model can be saturated or a projection, so a sponsor-facing
comparison must state the regime, history, outcome, and target population before interpreting
a number. The source review also records defaults that matter for reuse: a default GLM, a
cumulative probability floor of 0.01, and variance branches that can fall back to influence-curve
variance when the additional method is unavailable. With a supplied identifier, those influence
contributions are aggregated by household. These facts motivate a candidate native comparator
and exact contract tests; they do not establish native adoption or bank qualification. [292]
An exact public/generated construction makes the population issue visible. Let baseline risk H
have probability 1/3, let the first action A0 affect later hardship L1 , and let a binary default
outcome satisfy
                P (Y = 1 | H, A0 , L1 , A1 ) = 1/10 + H/10 + L1 /5 − A0 /25 − A1 /20.
The law stays between 0.01 and 0.40 over all histories. Hardship probabilities for (H, A0 ) =
(0, 0), (0, 1), (1, 0), (1, 1) are 1/4, 1/8, 3/4, 1/2. The status-quo (0, 0), targeted (H, L1 ), and broad-
contact (1, 1) regimes then give risks 13/60, 17/100, and 7/75. With USD 1,000 per default and
USD 6 and USD 4 per contact at the two times, their net values relative to status quo are USD
0, USD 130/3, and USD 340/3. Suppose outcome observation is conditionally independent of
default given history, with probabilities 9/10 for L1 = 0 and 3/4 for L1 = 1. Complete-case risks
under status quo and targeted servicing then become 69/335 and 139/850, with biases −43/4020
and −11/1700; known-probability inverse-probability weighting recovers the stipulated population
risks. These are exact witnesses for target, censoring, and missingness contracts, not fitted package
results or measured bank returns. [293]
The same source review finds household influence contributions summed within a household and
multiplied by the household-to-record count ratio; this does not silently turn a record-weighted
target into an equally weighted household target. Paired contrasts preserve their covariance,
while extreme probabilities, rank failures, nonconvergence, and unsupported variance requests
require explicit warnings or refusal. A future public prototype should replay the exact law, adverse
observation regimes, and covariance fixture, then compare a pinned native environment with the
proposal’s typed target and refusal ledger. Native engine rights, dependency closure, numerical
calibration, and a live bank workflow remain open. [292, 293]
The paired reader has 1,251 pages and 948 audited records, including 223 genuine scholarly PDFs.
This increment closes one selected reuse review and adds exact public/generated evidence; it
does not close the wider software coverage census, visual acceptance, independent review, bank
validation, or final investment decision. [2, 277]

12.1    Commercial execution services need a scientific result contract

requires Succeeded and an output commit. The actual supported server state space still needs
inspection. Posit’s R client distinguishes tasks, jobs and experimental scheduling, exposes bundle
and exit metadata with older-server omissions, and checks the final task code after completion.

documentation of those tests is not a passing trial. SAS’s partial Workflow client explicitly marks
itself experimental, converts naive prompt datetimes using host timezone with a fallback, and has
a score-polling path that can finish without an explicit timeout result. These source-level findings
justify typed adapter states, explicit clocks, observation deadlines and reconciliation before retries.
[297, 298, 299, 296]
Two stipulated monetary witnesses explain why this matters. Applying coefficients 3/100 and

are exact authored examples, not measured vendor failures, estimated effects or bank returns.

12.2    Structured causal models strengthen alternatives, not universal guaran-
        tees

Two additional genuine pre-cutoff papers narrow the research choices for dependent bank data.
Weinstein and Blei’s geometric causal models encode specified symmetries in causal mechanisms.
Their identification and consistency arguments retain known graph and symmetry, mixing, locality,
amenability and positivity conditions; the stated consistency result also restricts the relevant state
spaces. Their array example explicitly acknowledges poor absolute uncertainty calibration, and
their semisynthetic genomic comparison shows no uniformly best correction or symmetry choice.
This is a scoped research alternative, not a solution for arbitrary persistent bank dependence or
automated graph discovery. [303]
Aiad and coauthors describe a hierarchical pipeline from transformed graphs through pyAgrum
identification to a fitted expression tree. That architecture could help expose the boundary between
an identified formula and its actual numerical evaluation. The paper states that normalizing
unstable multiplicative factors changes the evaluated functional. Its mathematics effect of 36.80
and normalized reading diagnostic of 21.63 concern different outcomes, so they cannot isolate
normalization’s effect. The selected graph is plausible rather than uniquely recovered; deeper
hierarchies, uncertainty for stabilized functionals and evaluation across graph families remain
open. The linked package has not been newly inspected or qualified by this review. [304]
An exact banking illustration makes the target choice visible. Branches of 10 and 90 customers have
stipulated per-customer effects of USD 20 and USD -4, with USD 2 cost. Equal branch weighting
gives net USD 6; equal customer weighting gives USD -3.60. A separate nonconstant reweighting
changes an illustrative contrast from USD 2 to USD 5.60. Neither example estimates bank returns.
They require the product and any assisting agent to preserve intervention level, population weights
and every numerical alteration. Independent finite group and covariance witnesses also qualify
two apparent extracted proof slips without rejecting the papers’ full theorems. [305]
At that cited review stage, the following figures apply. The frontier census now contains 65
inspected and 48 unread originals of 113, with four crosscutting searches still open. The two
papers add bounded identification and computation alternatives; neither establishes live-model
superiority. The current documents retain earlier findings and separate source review, native

13     Panel estimators must preserve the business question

The current monograph integrates a detailed comparison of PyFixest and the original R synthdid
package. Fifteen authenticated PyFixest source files and thirteen synthdid files support specific
proposed conversion, numerical and inference contracts. The two packages remain reference
candidates with conditional routes to Python and R workers; no new native integration or bank
capability is claimed. The integrated reader preserves all 960 preceding audited citation identities
and adds four records, while retaining 225 genuine scholarly PDFs. [312, 313, 314]
A fixed-effect coefficient can answer a different question from the average benefit of an intervention.
In an exact constructed two-branch, three-period panel, all treated-cell effects are positive and
average USD 4, yet the two-way fixed-effect projection is USD -3.50. With an assumed USD 2
cost per treated cell, the target net is USD 2 while misreading the projection gives USD -5.50.
The example stipulates untreated outcomes and exposes the projection weights; it is not a fitted
PyFixest result or evidence of an observed bank loss. In a second example, weighting cohort
effects 10 and -2 by one-quarter and three-quarters gives 1 rather than the equal-cohort average 4.
Full covariance gives variance 91/16; omitting cross-covariance gives 85/16. [315]
The inspected Python interfaces require explicit period indexing, adoption dates, treatment coding,
first-stage support and cohort weighting. Calendar codes are not elapsed periods: 202601 minus
202512 is 89, whereas the month difference is one. Some methods retain a different event origin
or inference convention from what a generic event-study label might suggest. Native numerical
backends, fixed-effect removals, rank checks, small-sample choices and unsupported post-estimation
operations must be recorded for the selected method. [313]
The original synthdid package instead requires a complete balanced panel and simultaneous
treatment adoption. It estimates a treated-block contrast, not arbitrary staggered intervention
effects. A supplied-weight example gives USD 5.50 and an assumed USD 4 cost leaves USD
1.50. Incorrect control-weight normalization makes a common USD 100 post-period shock change
the contrast by USD 25, although valid normalized weights cancel it. These are independent
arithmetic oracles with supplied weights, not outputs from fitted native software. [314, 316]
Inference and recovery require equally explicit controls. Synthdid’s single-treated bootstrap and
jackknife refusals must remain missing uncertainty; a placebo alternative requires a separate
scientific justification. The inspected resampling loop has no attempt cap, and stored fitting
options omit two controls used by the original fit. A proposed worker must retain complete
options and report attempted, rejected and accepted draws with a deadline. Source inspection
identifies the questions for native replay; it does not establish a runtime failure, calibrated interval
or performance advantage. [314]
The next justified expenditure is a bounded public or synthetic prototype trial, with matched
targets and complete preparation, fitting, inference, failure and maintenance costs. Direct

Causal banking: product proposal                                                     23 October 2026


unrestricted adoption is deferred, and a cross-language port needs a measured benefit before
accepting duplicated numerical maintenance. Source licensing declarations and retained notice

16     Clarifying the proof boundary and the integration opportunity

The original factuality papers sharpen the preceding validation discussion. Mohri and Hashimoto
obtain a lower entailment-relative guarantee using sound back-off and a score that checks all
subsequent thresholds; nesting is additional for the tight upper bound. Their finite-sample theorem
restricts the error level to at least one over calibration size plus one. Their merger condition is
semantic entailment equivalence, not statistical independence among claims. Marginal validity
does not imply per-user, fixed-calibration or shifted-input validity. Rubin-Toles and colleagues
similarly distinguish a lower guarantee using actual subset-level coherent-factuality labels from
extra graph conditions for the tight upper bound. Graph-assisted silver labels are a cheaper
shortcut whose reliability must be checked against direct gold subset labels. Neither theory makes
a model’s confidence score into external truth. [331, 332]
The published StatsPAI paper describes a unified Python registry with method schemas, limitations,
stability tiers, structured failures and shared result surfaces for mature estimators, including
possible MCP use. It is a concrete comparator to our proposed integration layer. Before
building another broad wrapper, inspect an adapter against the proposal’s estimand, intervention,
population, clock, uncertainty and refusal contracts. The paper reports cross-language parity and
selected coverage studies, but also discloses narrower helper capabilities, optional-backend build


                                                37
Causal banking: product proposal                                                   23 October 2026


costs, simulated replicas and a synthetic-control local-optimum discrepancy. These are published
claims requiring exact code, data and harness review, not local native results. Its open-source
package and commercial downstream product are distinct. [333]
At that cited review stage, the following figures apply. The next decision is a bounded
adapter/reference evaluation, followed by direct adoption, an isolated worker, a selective port or
rejection if the scientific contract is lost. Three additional genuine papers are integrated; only
StatsPAI adds a census candidate, bringing the register to 68 inspected and 45 unread of 113,
with four crosscutting searches still open. The two proof originals are dependencies and do not
inflate that denominator. Exact current reader and progress bindings accompany this comparison.
[334, 335]


17     Choosing a causal adapter with an explicit scientific contract

The source-level StatsPAI comparison now distinguishes the paper’s archived version 1.23.0 from
cutoff source version 1.39.0. Eleven authenticated source files reveal useful registry, schema,
structured-error and replay mechanisms. They also show why the proposed service needs a stricter
consumer contract: the exported result schema requires only a method name, while the compact
MCP output schema accepts any object. Independent checks accept a deliberately reversed
interval, negative standard error and invalid probability; these are authored schema witnesses, not
observed estimator outputs. The service must bind target population, intervention, units, horizon,
assumptions and applicable uncertainty before accepting a result. [336, 337]
Operational guarantees also need exact scope. The caller distinguishes process execution from
thread fallback, and the latter may remain active after a timeout. The configured output budget
applies to structured content, which is also serialized in a text block; a 262,144-byte payload
contributes at least 524,288 bytes in two copies before envelope, escaping and image costs. Risk
truncation, nonfinite values, actual worker state and replay dependencies must survive the adapter.
Underlying helpers and native behavior remain unqualified. [336, 337]
The preferred route is a conditional isolated adapter with a source-reference disposition until
qualification. Direct blanket adoption is deferred; selective ports need a concrete scientific or
deployment benefit. Registry certification labels do not establish bank validity. Stored parity
fixtures must bind the estimator, input bytes, aggregation, statistic, convention, reference version
and tolerance. In our stipulated two-cohort example, customer weighting yields effect USD 0 and
net USD -1 after cost, while treated-observation weighting yields effect USD 2; substituting one
for the other changes the decision target. These are exact illustrative calculations, not measured
bank benefits. [336, 337]
The next steps are to inspect dependency notices and runtime helpers, select one locked estimator
environment, compare exact inputs and full uncertainty against a qualified reference, exercise
failure and restart cases, and compare integration cost with a direct mature-package wrapper.
Native execution, independent acceptance and bank adoption are later gates. The current paired
reader and canonical progress snapshot preserve the unresolved census and software obligations.
[338, 339]


18     Qualifying completion, custody and replay

Eight further authenticated StatsPAI helper files refine the adapter prerequisites. Process isolation
is opt-in and excludes several session-bound calls. The supervisor’s deadline starts after request
writing; its direct-child kill, delayed exit and EOF branches need native lifecycle tests before an
end-to-end completion guarantee. The default thread runner uses cooperative cancellation and

                                                 38
Causal banking: product proposal                                                 23 October 2026


may continue after returning a timeout. In a stipulated example, one benefit worth USD 10 and
one USD 7 attempt gives net USD 3; a duplicated charged attempt delivering the same one benefit
gives net USD -4. These inputs illustrate retry economics and are not measured costs. [340, 341]
The output budget explicitly permits unavoidable overflow when protected information cannot fit.
Cache byte eviction stops when one item remains, so a single oversized entry is possible. Path
checks, parsing and provenance hashing are separate operations, and metadata-based cache keys
are not content identities. The proposed service should stage immutable input bytes, track actual
worker ownership and decide how to handle an oversized artifact before dispatch. No race, memory
incident or native failure is alleged by this static review. [340, 341]
A standalone replay bundle improves custody by checking an input hash and reconstructing
transformations. Its generated comparison script nevertheless checks only selected point estimates
and can print success with zero comparisons; collected standard-error and sample-size fields are
not compared. The adapter must require a positive, complete comparison set covering uncertainty,
membership, weighting and diagnostics as well as estimates. Native trials, transformation/fixture
review and dependency/data rights remain separate gates. This source review strengthens the
proposal without granting implementation or adoption. The exact current reader and progress
snapshot accompany these conclusions. [340, 341, 342, 343]


19    A method-specific DoubleML route

Five authenticated Python DoubleML files at the 23 September 2026 revision clarify the proposed
worker’s scientific and licensing boundary. The interactive-regression route requires one binary
treatment and distinguishes ATE and ATTE targets. External nuisance predictions need saved
out-of-fold provenance; array shape does not establish honest training. Default covariates require
an explicit information-clock allowlist. The panel container’s first-adoption and static modes do
not establish arbitrary longitudinal inference. The cutoff checkout and the previously observed
installed version 0.11.4 remain different evidence objects. [344]
An independently authored fixed-nuisance example produces four score contributions 6, -2, 4 and
0, with mean 2 and stated empirical iid variance of the mean 5/2. At a stipulated USD 10 per
effect unit and cost USD 15, net value is USD 5. A separate residual contribution changes from
1000 to 100 when a propensity is clipped from 0.001 to 0.01. This arithmetic illustrates the score
contract; it grants neither finite-sample coverage nor restored overlap. No upstream package code
is executed. [345]
Retain the package as a method-specific reference and qualify an isolated IRM adapter only after
inspecting its propensity, solver, split-aggregation and covariance delegates, binding a complete
release and environment, reviewing dependency rights and replaying the selected registered
protocol. The BSD notice does not clear every dependency or dataset. A new language port needs
a measured maintenance or platform advantage. The previously recorded reuse decision remains
inconclusive: this source follow-up does not supply the missing complete artifact, dependency lock
and original license inventory. The paired reader and current coverage snapshot preserve that
distinction. [344, 346, 347]


20    Sensitivity that can change an investment decision

Two source-grounded reference decisions cover sensemakr 0.1.6 and EValue 4.1.4. Thirteen
authenticated source and documentation blobs distinguish regression partial-R-squared sensitivity
from risk-ratio thresholds and approximate effect conversions. Neither reference decision grants
an implemented adapter. The inspected sensemakr fixest path explicitly uses iid covariance, so

                                               39
Causal banking: product proposal                                                   23 October 2026


a clustered model cannot silently retain its original uncertainty through that path. Inclusive
input guards, singular denominators, differing helper/wrapper interval degrees of freedom and
benchmark capping remain native qualification cases. The extra estimate argument is accepted
through ellipsis; it is not an argument-error finding. [348]
With stipulated regression coefficient 3, standard error 1, residual degrees of freedom 8 and partial
R-squared restrictions 1/5 and 1/2, exact bias is 1, adjusted coefficient is 2 and adjusted variance
is 5/7. Valuing one coefficient unit at USD 10 with cost USD 25 changes point-based net from
USD 5 to USD -5. This authored example shows why sensitivity can reverse a decision; it is
neither measured benefit nor calibrated uncertainty. [349]
EValue’s reciprocal risk ratios 9/5 and 5/9 give threshold 3, while an interval crossing the target
gives threshold 1. The number is not a probability that the causal claim is true. Its nonrare
odds-ratio conversion is approximate: for odds ratio 81/25 it gives 9/5, whereas a stipulated
baseline risk 1/5 yields exact risk ratio 405/181. A proposed worker must preserve event coding,
target, original interval provenance, rare-outcome flags and full conversion history. Complete
dependency custody, applicable delivery rights, native boundary tests and scientific justification

21     Clarify the business goal before exposing tools

A request to improve a distressed-loan portfolio can mean changing contact frequency, offering
payment holidays or explaining existing arrears. Even a valid causal estimator answers the
wrong question when it receives the wrong intervention. GIST-CMTF supplies a concrete design
comparator: infer a symbolic goal, assess ambiguity and expose a clarification action before
dispatching downstream tools. Its state-transition graph represents declared tool preconditions
and effects; it does not establish a borrower causal diagram, exchangeability or an identified
treatment effect. A banking proposal should retain an explicit intervention, comparator, population,
outcome, horizon, information clock and owner after clarification. [353, 354]
The authentic ten-page original reports 120 controlled tasks, six methods and seven model backends.
Independent aggregation of the released 5,040 final rows reproduces 815 successes for GIST-CMTF
versus 673 for top-goal CMTF, each out of 840. The corresponding wrong-goal counts are 21 and
163. The request-type breakdown qualifies that headline: explicit-goal success is lower than the
semantic comparator and 16.8% of explicit requests trigger unnecessary clarification. These are
reported benchmark results and reaggregation of saved outputs, with deterministic mocked tool
effects. They are not new model calls or evidence of banking superiority. [353, 354, 355]
Eleven artifacts are authenticated against the repository revision dated 30 June 2026. Their
provenance does not yet support direct replay of the published experiment. The runner uses
annotated request type and substitutes the intended goal after clarification; 53 of 560 nonexplicit
GIST rows instead report no clarification. The runner expects a gold-path field absent from
all 120 released tasks, and its output schema differs from the saved CSV. A different producer
or transformation may explain these observations, but the generating revision and cleaning log
remain unresolved. The paper acknowledges that real user dialogue, latency, monetary cost
and satisfaction are unmeasured. None of these findings is an allegation of fabricated results.
[353, 354, 355]

Causal banking: product proposal                                                     23 October 2026


The proposed next experiment should compare a label-free goal classifier, a clarification policy,
a lexical baseline and a named oracle on frozen held-out public or generated business requests.
Actual clarification responses, wrong goals, abstentions, retries and provider failures belong in the
full denominator. For an illustrative success value of USD 10 and token cost of USD 0.001, the
released descriptive averages imply a net difference of 388021/420000 dollars at clarification cost
USD 2 and -163979/420000 dollars at cost USD 6. Those stipulated inputs expose an economic
threshold; they do not measure bank ROI or a causal performance gain. [355]

23     Separate checked derivations from supported premises

An agent can execute a valid collection rule after misreading a borrower’s record. CPUNeSy
provides a useful design comparator for controlling which model proposals enter a symbolic state.
Its authentic 37-page September 2026 original separates source admission, restricted predicate
writes, derivation checking and abstention. The source-soundness theorem assumes a sound source
checker; the paper explicitly says that assumption has not been established for the experimental
natural-language checkers. Its 2027 conference running header is not independently verified
acceptance at the literature cutoff. [362]
The proposed banking interface should report source support and formal validity separately.
Restricting writable source predicates can stop a model from inserting the desired conclusion
directly, but a false allowed leaf can still imply that conclusion under a valid rule. An independent
toy example makes that limitation explicit. Likewise, two agreeing model calls do not establish
independence or semantic truth. With stipulated shared-error mass 1/5 and independent slip
probability 1/10 elsewhere, two-vote answered risk is 26/107 and repeated voting retains an error
floor of 1/5. [362, 363]
The reported experiments distinguish execution, checking and fallback. The uncertified solver
achieves 98.8% full-pool accuracy on L1-HARD versus 97.1% for restricted two-vote serving;
checking is a selective-serving choice, not a universal accuracy improvement. With DeepSeek
ContractNLI traces fixed, source rechecking removes 63 wrong answers and withholds 51 correct
answers, adding 114 abstentions among 2,091 items. Fallback answers are explicitly uncertified.
Historical adapter rows appearing in the main table are described in the appendix as unmatched
summaries without exact split/model denominators; those rows cannot support a matched
superiority ranking. [362, 363]
For stated wrong-answer cost USD 10 and added checking cost USD 100, the printed changes imply
loss savings USD 302 at abstention cost USD 2 and USD-154 at cost USD 6. These are stipulated
examples, not measured bank value. A prospective public benchmark should freeze source spans,
rule vocabulary, held-out labels and serving policies; inject both forbidden target writes and
false permitted leaves; and retain full retry, abstention, fallback and certificate records. The
original’s archive custody, immutable model revisions, prospective calibration and physical-storage
accounting remain incomplete here. [362, 363]
At that cited review stage, the following figures apply. The controlled-write predecessor contains
992 audited citations and 232 genuine scholarly PDFs. This source review advances the original
