grants no native integration, semantic-truth certificate or banking capability. [364, 365]


24     Require a policy evaluator to preserve the business decision

SCOPE-RL 0.2.1 is a useful reference for offline servicing-policy evaluation, but its inspected source
cannot yet be admitted as a banking safety evaluator. Eleven authenticated files at the selected
December 2023 revision expose the episode layout, logging and target probabilities, fitted-value
preparation, interval helpers and policy-ranking contracts. The proposal retains this profile as a
reference. Full adoption and runtime integration remain deferred; porting to another language has
no demonstrated advantage sufficient to justify replicating these contracts and their maintenance.
[366]
Even a small conversion error can change the investment decision. The default high-level evaluator
masks the reward on the done row itself. If the flag means that the current transition just delivered
a terminal receipt, rewards USD 2 and USD 8 become USD 2 instead of USD 10. At stipulated


                                                 42
Causal banking: product proposal                                                    23 October 2026


service cost USD 6, net value becomes USD -4 instead of USD 4. A different flag convention may
justify the mask; the proposed adapter must define and test the actual clock. These are exact
authored examples, not measured package or banking results. [366, 367]
The interval names also require source review. The Hoeffding-named expression multiplies by
sample size where its adjacent display divides by it, uses an observed maximum rather than a
declared population range, and can reverse its bounds for negative constant scores. The Bernstein-
named expression has a different variance scaling from its display. The Student helper returns
symmetric endpoints using a one-sided quantile: under the exact normal-sample pivot model, a
95-percent label corresponds to 90-percent central coverage. The fixed-score bootstrap does not
refit learned quantities or recompute a self-normalized denominator. In an exact two-episode
example, fixed-score and recomputed-ratio bootstrap variances are 9/2 and 9/4. None of these
witnesses is an empirical coverage trial of the package. [366, 367]
The selected lower-bound ranking path requires on-policy return arrays even when metrics are
omitted, which makes it a benchmark interface rather than a ready logged-only banking service.
Its selected calls do not adjust alpha across candidate policies, and a lower-quartile helper
needs an explicit policy-name/value pairing repair. A public prototype should use finite known
trajectory laws, adverse terminal/reward conventions, support failures, signed and degenerate
returns, shared calendar shocks, and held-out policy selection. It must preserve policy identity and
refuse unsupported requests consistently through the library, CLI and MCP interfaces. [366, 367]
The required route is concrete: bind the complete release and actual dependency versions and
rights; review the time, interval and selection repairs; then run registered native fixtures in that
environment. The source license declaration does not establish transitive clearance. Scientific
logging truth, selected-policy uncertainty, measured workflow cost and institutional adoption
remain separate obligations. The SCOPE-RL predecessor has 994 audited citations and 232
genuine scholarly PDFs. At that review the reuse register had 43 passing proposal dispositions,
three inconclusive and 69 open entries; these counts do not grant native capabilities. [366, 368, 369]


25     Preserve the one-decision target when reusing a bandit library

Open Bandit Pipeline 0.5.5 provides a source-qualified reference for one-time offer allocation.
Its inspected contextual-bandit estimators, regression and tuning wrappers do not replace a
longitudinal servicing analysis. Eleven authenticated files establish a concrete profile at the
selected November 2022 revision. The proposal retains this profile as a reference; complete
adoption, runtime integration and broad porting remain unqualified. [370]
Correct logging probabilities materially affect the business decision. In an exact two-context
example, the target always gives offer one and has value 7/2. Clipping the importance ratio at
two gives 3, while substituting uniform logging probabilities gives 17/4. At an assumed USD
10 per reward unit and USD 32 offer cost, the margins are USD 3, USD -2 and USD 10.50.
Deterministic replay can also produce 17/4 here because unequal logging changes the composition
of matched contexts. For a stochastic target with no exact-one action matches, the selected replay
source leaves zero scores; the proposed product must report unavailable replay evidence instead of
interpreting that as zero policy value. [370, 371]
The core validators and preparation paths need a strict adapter contract. Probability rows must be
finite and nonnegative as well as sum to one; row, action and position identities must agree. The
regression wrapper’s uniform propensity fallback is not authenticated logging truth. Its default fit
is in-sample, while its multi-fold alternative shuffles rows without imposing customer groups or
calendar separation. The interval helper resamples fixed scores, and a tuned estimator retains its
selected threshold on the object. Those operations do not by themselves account for all fitting,


                                                 43
Causal banking: product proposal                                                    23 October 2026


selection or dependence uncertainty. [370]
The source review also rejects an apparent defect: the tuned interval’s omitted estimated-propensity
constructor flag does not establish a wrong-array error, because the chosen propensity array is
already forwarded as the base estimator’s ordinary input. OBP’s Student helper is a one-sided
lower bound, so its quantile must not inherit the symmetric-interval criticism from SCOPE-RL.
This distinction keeps review findings tied to actual contracts. [370, 371]
The public-data route is useful but bounded. The inspected Open Bandit Dataset class compares
empirical campaign reward means, sorts by timestamp and prepares features before optional
chronological splitting. That is observable benchmark evidence with sampling and population
limits, not exact counterfactual truth or bank validation. The prototype should use known finite
laws and adverse tests for logging imbalance, clipping, no matches, invalid probabilities, position
permutations, repeated customers and tuned-object reuse. Dataset rights, actual dependencies,
native outputs and costs still require review and measurement. Library, CLI and MCP requests
must preserve these same target and refusal rules. [370, 371]
The OBP predecessor has 996 audited citations and 232 genuine scholarly PDFs. At that review
there were 44 passing proposal reuse dispositions, three inconclusive and 68 open entries; the

preserving the remaining scientific and operating gates. [372, 373]


26     Treat hidden-state planning as a supplied-model service

POMDP-Py 1.3.5.1 provides useful model, belief, history and planner interfaces, but a search result
cannot establish that a bank’s hidden-state model is identified or its observation law is correct.
Thirteen authenticated files at the selected October 2025 revision support a reference-only reuse
decision. Complete adoption, native integration and an unrestricted optimal-policy uncertainty
claim remain unqualified. [374]
An exact example shows why the observation law matters. With prior distress probability 1/4
and a stipulated diagnostic sensor, posterior distress is 1/2 after a positive signal and 1/10 after a
negative signal. A rescue action worth USD 35 in distress and USD -15 otherwise gives expected
net USD 7/4 when used only after a positive signal, including a USD 2 test cost. If the actual
sensor is uninformative, the same policy gives USD -13/4. Better search of an incorrect sensor
model cannot repair that scientific gap. These are authored finite-law examples, not measured
banking or package performance. [375]
The inspected numerical contracts need explicit qualification. An impossible observation can
leave a zero-mass histogram; a restricted next-state set can silently renormalize away omitted
support. Particle depletion is an explicit failure, and repeating survivors does not create new
evidence. Weighted duplicate-state probability queries and sampling can imply probabilities 1/2
and 2/3 in a simple example, while ordinary unweighted particles override those paths and preserve
frequency 2/3. The proposed worker must distinguish these representations and their failure
modes. [374, 375]
The planner’s outer time check runs between simulations. A rollout repeatedly choosing a zero-
duration option can fail to advance, so an independent deadline is required. Option reward clocks
also matter: a two-step example gives value 15 under undiscounted internal rewards versus 11
under primitive-step discounting. The finite policy-tree recurrence gives 13/8 where ordinary
one-step discounting gives 7/4 in a three-stage unit-reward example. The alternative small-state
value routine uses one-step discounting under its own finite deterministic assumptions. These are
static and exact-reference findings, with no native execution claim. [374, 375]
The prototype route is concrete: bind complete model/source/compiler/runtime identities and

                                                 44
Causal banking: product proposal                                                   23 October 2026


rights; resolve belief, history, duration, arity and horizon contracts; run independent finite-state
and adverse fixtures; then measure search quality, time, memory and recovery. Library, CLI
and MCP must return the same supplied-model target, proposed action, diagnostics and refusal
reasons. An LLM can help formulate candidate states or diagrams, but cannot authenticate the
hidden observation law by supplying a plausible description. [374]
The POMDP-Py paired reader had 998 audited citations and 232 genuine scholarly PDFs. At
the POMDP-Py review there were 45 passing proposal dispositions, three inconclusive and 67
open entries. These decisions narrow prototype choices while leaving native capability, scientific
qualification, bank validation and adoption separate. [376, 377]


27     Preserve the decision experiment across a Julia worker

POMDPs.jl 1.0.0 offers a useful root interface for models, solvers, policies, belief updaters and
simulators. Sixteen authenticated files at the selected 8 June 2026 revision support a reference-only
disposition. The root license explicitly separates other organization packages, so neither a solver
nor POMDPTools inherits approval. A concrete worker still needs a selected algorithm, updater
and simulator, their source and rights, and a locked Julia environment. [378]
The proposed integration must preserve one joint decision event. In an exact authored example,
next-state distress and a matching alert share an event with probability 1/4. Splitting generator
outputs into independent calls preserves the marginals but reduces state-alert agreement from 1
to 5/8 and alert-loss probability from 1/4 to 1/16. A stipulated alert-triggered rescue worth USD
35 in distress and USD -15 otherwise then changes expected incremental value from USD 35/4 to
USD -5/8. This is a counterexample to an adapter strategy, not an observed upstream failure or a
measured bank return. [379]
Other conversion contracts are equally substantive. A solver’s observation signature must support
the model’s dependence on the previous state. A narrower reward signature must return a
probability-weighted expectation: the example gives USD -4, not the USD -16 uniform state
average. Zero terminal continuation does not erase a USD 12 settlement paid on entry. Generalized
beliefs may be lossy histories rather than normalized posteriors, and pass-through initialization
does not establish sufficiency or an isolated snapshot. [378, 379]
The prototype route is to preserve model and event identities, clock, horizon, reward units and
random-stream protocol across library, CLI and MCP; register finite-oracle conversion checks; and
measure offline solve, online action, compilation, serialization, accuracy, memory and recovery
separately. An LLM can draft a candidate model, but scientific review must justify its latent state
and intervention/observation laws. Neither the API nor the generated description establishes
identification or calibrated policy uncertainty. [378]
At the Julia interface review the paired reader had 1000 audited citations and 232 genuine scholarly
PDFs. The reuse register then had 46 passing proposal dispositions, three inconclusive and 66
open entries. Source review narrows a prototype choice while leaving native qualification, wider
proposal coverage, visual review and institutional acceptance open. [380, 381]


28    Keep model surgery and prediction separate in a Bayesian
      worker

PyMC supplies useful machinery for a bank-specific probability model, but a causal proposal
must state which mechanism is replaced and which variables are generated afterward. Twelve
authenticated source, documentation and test files at the selected 7 October 2026 development


                                                 45
Causal banking: product proposal                                                    23 October 2026


revision support a reference-only disposition. The version is dynamically derived; this is not
a verified named release. Its Python and PyTensor ranges, root Apache-2.0 terms and actual
dependency artifacts belong in a future locked worker environment. [382]
Conditioning on a treatment differs from replacing its assignment mechanism. In an exact
authored model with hidden borrower type, the observed association is four outcome units but
the intervention effect is two. At a stipulated USD 100 per unit and action cost USD 250, an
association-based calculation reports net USD 150 while the causal model gives USD -50. Two
other structural models produce exactly the same observed law while allowing effects one and zero.
Posterior computation can be numerically accurate while the model choice remains unresolved
by those observations. These are proposal witnesses, not package benchmarks or observed bank
returns. [383]
Prediction needs an explicit trace contract. Output selection does not request regeneration; stored
random variables can remain frozen after upstream data change. Replacing a deterministic square
by a cube can also reuse stored values unless regeneration is requested. The illustrative two-point
means are 5/2 and 9/2. Potential factors need separate attention because they are not generative
rules, although posterior ancestors may already encode their effects. Model and expression identity,
frozen variables and regenerated variables must therefore travel together across library, CLI and
MCP. [382, 383]
A prototype should pin the sampler and backend as well as the model. Conditional default selection
can change the numerical engine; external samplers reject some trace and callback paths. Missing
divergence or trajectory-depth statistics produce no corresponding warning, so diagnostic absence
must remain distinct from an inspected satisfactory result. Register finite and conjugate oracles,
intervention/prediction checks, warnings, effective samples, compilation, memory and persistence
before claiming native qualification. Scientific owners still need to justify causal assumptions and
policy uncertainty. An LLM may draft a model, but neither the generated graph nor a successful
sampler certifies it. [382]

29     Preserve latent uncertainty when a compiled worker recom-
       mends an action

NumPyro offers a conditional Python-worker route for a supplied probability model. Twenty
authenticated primary files at the selected 5 October 2026 revision declare version 0.22.0 and
document handlers, prediction, discrete inference and numerical execution. This authenticates a
source declaration, not a distributed release or a performance advantage. Python, JAX, jaxlib,
the optional Funsor route and actual device artifacts require a locked environment and separate
rights custody. The root license is Apache-2.0; the differing license filenames in metadata and the
tree require inspection of actual distribution notices before release. [386]
Observation, intervention and replay move values with different meanings. In the inspected
intervention handler, the sampled value retained under the original treatment name can differ
from the treatment value propagated to descendants. An interface must preserve both roles. The
independent finite reference gives an association of four response units and an intervention effect of
two under one supplied confounded model. Two further constructions have the same intervention
success marginals of one half but cross-world conditional success probabilities of one and zero. A
sampler cannot resolve that missing structural coupling from the shared marginals. [386, 387]


                                                 46
Causal banking: product proposal                                                    23 October 2026


A second exact example connects hidden-state uncertainty to the banking decision. A customer
alert has probability 5/12 and changes the probability of a high-benefit customer type from 1/3 to
3/5. With stipulated action payoffs of USD 100 for that type and USD -200 otherwise, posterior
averaging values the action at USD -20 and selects no action. Substituting the most probable type
instead reports USD 100 and selects the action, losing an expected USD 20 under the specified
posterior law. These are authored proposal amounts, not bank returns or an observed NumPyro
failure. [387]
The proposed worker must distinguish posterior state samples from MAP decoding, stored latent
values from regenerated outcomes, deterministic recomputation from substitution, and output
selection from all of these. The inspected prediction default recomputes deterministic sites;
PyMC’s different predictive controls remain a substantive alternative. Discrete inference has
additional support, plate and block constraints. Its scan documentation warns of unsupported
use, while selected unexecuted tests include lengths one and two and mark length ten as expected
to fail. A registered construction-specific trial must resolve the relevant boundary. [386, 382]
Before native approval, compare finite and conjugate oracles, check actual device use and precision,
retain random-key lineage and diagnostic availability, and fault-test complete sampler-state
recovery. Compilation, synchronized warmup, useful posterior samples, prediction, memory and
persistence all belong in the cost comparison. A broad language port would duplicate these
semantics without a measured benefit. Library, CLI and MCP should carry the same experiment
and uncertainty declarations; an LLM can draft them, while scientific owners must justify the
causal assumptions. [386, 387]

31     Dataframe speed must preserve the banking question

Polars receives a source-qualified reference disposition from twenty-one authenticated files at a
revision before the 7 October 2026 cutoff. The Python source declares 2.0.0 and its matching
runtime; the Rust workspace declares 0.55.1. Distributed releases and native conformance are not
authenticated by those declarations. The proposed worker preserves customer membership, join
cardinality, missing values, information time, ordering and the selected execution plan. [396]
Exact authored examples expose why those contracts affect a decision. Event duplication changes a
supplied customer contrast from 5 to 7.5; against a stipulated USD 6 threshold, descriptive margins
change from minus USD 1 to USD 1.50. Neither contrast is established as causal. Observed
outcomes 2 and 4 with one missing value permit full-population means 2 and 14/3. A historical
event that arrived after the decision is ineligible even when a backward event-time join selects it.
Overlapping windows have variance 3/8 where an independence calculation gives 1/4. These are
supplied examples, not package failures or bank measurements. [397]
Following the Python documentation into Rust resolves a narrower execution question: checked join
cardinality bypasses a specialized streaming path and can create an accumulating in-memory join.
Selecting streaming therefore cannot by itself establish bounded memory. Removing validation
to improve speed changes the accepted-input contract. A future matched trial must include
validation, input scans, fallback, conversion, output checks and recovery in its costs. [396]

connect other languages through the common CLI/MCP contract after conformance work. At the

open entries. At the Polars review the paired reader had 1010 audited citations and 232 genuine

33     A stability score cannot certify a causal answer

A genuine 24-page preprint proposes a combined sensitivity and entropy score for abstention and
training. Its perturbation diagnostic is relevant to business-question translation, but its general risk
guarantees do not survive explicit mathematical checks. The source’s expected sensitivity is below
its worst-case sensitivity; replacing the latter in an upper bound requires additional justification.
Its entropy subtraction and conditional-selection steps also fail in admissible examples. [405]
A constant fair binary model has self-risk one-half, while the source’s claimed bound can equal
one-half minus log two, a negative number. A separate bounded constant-loss example contradicts
the earlier bound. Selecting only a stable but hard tenth of inputs gives conditional self-risk one-
half against an unconditional one-twentieth. These are authored mathematical counterexamples,
not model trials. Equal entropy and output stability can also coexist with true classification error
nine-tenths. [406]
Retain sensitivity and entropy as logged diagnostics for prospective held-out comparison at
matched coverage and complete cost. Reject the generic certification as stated. Reported empirical
tables remain unverified until model, data, split, sample and run identities are supplied. The
original is retained with adverse findings; no allegation of fabricated experiments or claim of native
failure follows. [405]

35.8    Observation contracts: what a validation pass actually buys

Pandera is a candidate substrate for episode keys, declared units and information-availability rules.
The inspected cutoff source distinguishes disabled validation, skipped check scopes, sampled data,
warning-only failures and completed checks. It can also transform the table through defaults,
coercion, filtering and row removal. Native Polars lazy inputs have a different default check depth
from eager inputs, and optional backend dispatch changes the implementation. A pass flag alone
cannot carry this meaning. The proposed worker binds input, schema, effective configuration,
checks run or skipped, failures, transformations and returned artifact. No native integration is
admitted. [493]
Exact project-owned examples explain the economic and scientific stakes. A uniform sample of ten
from one hundred records with two violations misses both with probability 89/110. A selection
rule in a randomized zero-effect example creates a retained observational contrast of one. Two
structural worlds share the same valid-looking observed data but have intervention effects one and

35.9    Dynamic panels require a moment and calendar contract

The pydynpd review supplies a concrete Python candidate for restricted linear dynamic-panel
analysis. The genuine 2023 original describes difference/system GMM and automatic model
search; its historical benchmarks do not establish current commercial parity. Primary source
inspection identifies an ordering contract in panel construction, categorical time spacing, distinct
estimator/diagnostic steps, pseudoinverse and nominal instrument-count conventions, skipped
search exceptions and an iterative loop without an explicit cap. These are source-level findings,
not observed native failures. The current disposition is reference-only, with a conditional original-
worker route after qualification. [497, 498]
The scientific contract comes first. Lag moments require justified orthogonality, system moments
add initial-condition restrictions, and high persistence can weaken instruments. A correctly
solved invalid moment can return coefficient two when the structural coefficient is one. Repeated
regressors can identify only their sum. A common shock replicated across one hundred accounts
retains variance one although diagonal-only accounting suggests 1/100. These exact examples
explain why rank, independence and nonrejected diagnostics must not be inferred from a regression
table. [499, 500, 501]
The proposed library/CLI/MCP worker binds immutable data, a regular calendar and sorted
keys, explicit instruments, covariance and diagnostic steps, the entire candidate-search ledger,
and bounded termination. Generated/public panels should test row-permutation invariance, gaps,
invalid/weak moments, missingness, common shocks and failed searches before bank trials. A
targeted repair is preferable to an unqualified wholesale port; direct MIT metadata does not
resolve the full environment or rights. Source version declarations disagree, so the exact commit
remains the review identity. [498]
Economic interpretation also needs a declared regime. An immediate USD 10 response implies USD
50 at persistence 0.8 and USD 200 at 0.95 under the stipulated stable linear model; uncertainty
reaching persistence one prevents a bounded plug-in long-run claim. In a separate USD 100
accounting example, twenty attempts, eight diagnostic survivors and two accepted artifacts imply

are authored examples and motivate a matched study, not observed bank returns. [501]

35.10    A scientific loop with testable boundaries

The May 2025 Zhang and colleagues perspective organizes scientific assistance around observation,
hypothesis, experiment and automation. Its 45-page preprint supports that organizing argument
and its cautions about reasoning, translation and trust. The related journal record has a different
title; its original remains unavailable, so the cited version is the actual preprint. Descriptions of
underlying tools are leads for separate primary review. [504]
An assistant can solve the wrong problem faithfully. Extracting eventual repayment loses the
difference between USD 10,000 repaid on day 5 and on day 25 when the decision concerns ten-day
cash. A formally checked cost constraint of at most USD 100 fails an intended constraint strictly
below USD 100 at the boundary. Exact authored examples also show majority error 0.00856
for five independent judges with individual error 0.1, but error 0.1 when they share the same
mistake. Agreement is therefore not a general scientific-confidence guarantee. These are stipulated
mathematical witnesses, not measured model failures. [505]
The proposed prototype compares fixed workflows, retrieval assistance and bounded planning
on frozen public/generated tasks, using independent answer/refusal oracles, complete failures
and fixed search budgets. Public evidence can justify the merit of later bank experiments. It
cannot establish transport by itself: a stipulated public choice yielding USD 8 rather than USD
3 becomes USD -4 rather than USD 3 in another environment, giving USD 7 regret. Question,
diagram, identification and experiment artifacts should share exact targets and provenance through
CLI/MCP calls. This is a proposed contract, not implemented integration. [504, 505]
A stipulated USD 240 workflow across 40 attempts, 30 tool completions and eight accepted answers
costs USD 6, USD 8 and USD 30 respectively. Six correct refusals remain a separate outcome.
Such complete accounting makes a prototype investment assessable without claiming bank ROI,

provenance and refuse unsupported abduction.[743]

examples distinguish that law from an ordinary bootstrap. Dictionary summaries additionally
need fixed schemas and repetition IDs, because equal per-key counts can hide disjoint repetitions.

46.19    Graph refinement needs a decision and measurement clock

The pediatric ECMO study offers a concrete hybrid pattern: experts select variables and forbidden
directions, language models propose a graph, and data guide deletion-only and full refinement.
The inspected five-page author original reports 71 patients and eight Boolean variables. It also
reports adverse comparisons: the union graph’s mean SHD rises from 5.4 after deletion to 6.6 after
full refinement. Agreement with an expert graph and plausible added edges are useful observations,
but neither supplies independent causal truth or bank decision value. [786]
Our proposal adopts this as a reference for elicitation, with a separate decision on identification.
A monthly table can hide whether a hardship contact preceded a missed payment. The prototype
must preserve event and observation times, distinguish constraints from evidence, and refuse an
unsupported causal answer. Public or generated cases with known mechanisms can compare
expert-only, data-only, LLM-only, deletion and full-refinement routes before expensive bank
experiments. [787]
Static inspection of ten pinned source files adds test requirements for case identity, graph conversion
and complete denominators. In a conditional example, six score-8 files in ten zero-initialized slots
yield a mean of 4.8 instead of the observed-only mean of 8; no actual missing archive file was
observed. The repository states a ten-bootstrap profile and omits patient data. Native execution,
installed-distribution rights, supplementary prompt and model identities, and the final publisher
layout remain separate work. [788, 787]

46.20    A Java interface must preserve the panel estimand

distinction. [789, 790, 791]
An exact constructed two-group, three-period example has positive treatment effects averaging

regression target’s weights, not a failed numerical solver. A second example gives variance 1/2 for
two repeated cluster shocks versus 1/4 for four independent rows. Public tests must check the
estimand, sampling law and unsupported horizons separately from numeric agreement. Unknown
counterfactual trends require additional assumptions or information. [792]

panel worker, preserving treatment history, control rules, identities, weights and uncertainty.

46.21    C++ acceleration must retain panel semantics

The C++ comparison follows selected fixest kernels together with their R callers and a dated
xhdfe development profile. Residualization, rank, row ordering, covariance scaling and unavailable
inference are part of the contract. A port that copies only a numerical kernel can lose those
meanings. The proposed initial fixest route remains a typed worker preserving the original
package’s semantics. [793, 156]
xhdfe supplies explicit cluster, convergence, selection and covariance-repair fields, which make it
a useful public-profile candidate. Static inspection also motivates a full-matrix covariance test:
projecting a full matrix and then replacing a separately projected slope block is not generally
guaranteed to preserve positive semidefiniteness. Our exact counterexample has determinant -3/4
and a contrast variance of -3. It does not prove that this output is attainable in the package, and
no native failure was observed. Reachability, scientific coverage and cost remain separate tests.
[794, 795]
Four further leads are retained separately: SDID, capybara, gfe and NNRPanel. Their dated scope
and dependency declarations differ; implementation and underlying scholarly claims remain to be
inspected. The gfe license-field placeholder conflicts with the root MIT text and needs resolution.
Root licenses do not clear full distributions. [796, 797, 798, 799]

52.1    Shared-state inference and remaining-language network contracts

Shared servicing capacity creates interference even when a fixed borrower graph is inadequate.
The selected Hays–Raghavan author paper distinguishes an immediate direct effect from the global

independent auxiliary training, overlap, moments and nuisance rates. A fitted forest or simulated
queue does not establish those conditions. [921]

sources need reconciliation before adoption. Source and paper use different displayed switchback
windows; exact authored fair-block enumeration gives probabilities 7/16 and 3/8 for the respective
two- and three-observation events. Other expressions differ in IPW normalization, variance scaling,
lag bounds and comparator bookkeeping. These are static findings with authored witnesses, not
measured package failure rates or a rejection of the paper theorem. The selected tree has no
license-named original and its installed environment remains unqualified. [922, 923]

52.2     R mediation and sensitivity: preserving the business question

A servicing intervention can affect loss through engagement, liquidity or other mechanisms.
Ordinary treatment effects, natural mediation effects and randomized-mediator effects require
different assumptions and can differ under an exactly known law. The selected primary texts
provide explicit conditions and estimation routes; Bayesian or semiparametric software output
does not establish those conditions. [929, 930]
Six R comparisons extend the earlier mediation, medflex, sensemakr and EValue work. CMAverse
offers a broad interface, but its selected post-treatment g-formula path globally permutes mediator
columns; conditional and joint mediator laws require reconciliation. medoutcon offers efficient
scores and two-phase measurement corrections, with weight-normalization, constant-outcome and
sampling-law contracts requiring qualification. regmedint supplies conditional interaction-aware
comparisons whose covariate target and rare-outcome restrictions remain visible. [931, 932, 933]
causalsens and tipr address sensitivity scenarios with different units, signs and scales. Reuse
depends on preserving the requested scenario and numerical boundary. An odds ratio cannot
silently be treated as a risk ratio merely because both are positive. [934, 935]
BayesGmed’s selected sensitivity sources use the zero-state probability in both latent-state
likelihood terms. The inspected generated C++ retains this discrepancy. Exact authored
enumeration shows a non-normalized mixture and a changed model even after renormalization.
This source finding is not a measured native failure rate and does not reject ordinary models
or Bayesian mediation generally. Prior, link and license-scope issues also require reconciliation.
[936, 937]
The investment route qualifies independent public known-law fixtures before expensive bank
experiments, retaining complete failures, scientific refusals and workflow costs. CLI and MCP
contracts carry causal targets, conditioning, scale, sampling units and sensitivity parameters.
Agents can help elicit declarations, but cannot supply scientific truth. The bounded search exposes
105 unique leads and explicit uninspected tails; coverage remains inconclusive and no native
capability is admitted. [937]

54.5    Observation data and workflow execution as a scientific contract

The proposed product needs an observation contract and an execution contract before it can
make a credible claim about causal results. A financial unit, source record, proposed identity
match and physical job attempt must remain distinct. The selected data.table, targets, Dask,
Snakemake, Beam, Arrow, Taskflow, DataFrames.jl and Dagger implementations provide useful

bounded comparisons; five discovery queries do not establish complete market coverage, and no
downloaded implementation was executed. [995, 996, 997, 998, 999, 1000, 1001, 1002, 1003, 1004]
A two-by-two duplicate-key join can double USD300 of loan exposure to USD600 while remaining
at a row-expansion limit. An event-time lookup can select a revision unavailable at the decision
date. In a separate authored linkage example, an 80% match probability between possible losses of
USD100 and USD1,000 gives expected loss USD280, not the USD100 top-match plug-in. Applying
that plug-in to 10,000 hypothetical accounts understates expected loss by USD1.8 million. These
are reproducible constructed consequences, not bank loss estimates or observed package defects.
They justify public tests of cardinality, clocks, missing identities and retained linkage uncertainty.
[1004]
The preferred architecture conditionally adopts mature engines behind explicit worker contracts.
R pairs tabular workers with targets where appropriate; Python compares task-graph and file-
workflow roles; JVM workers declare their Beam/Spark runner and sink; C++ separates Arrow
data representation from Taskflow execution; Julia separates DataFrames joins from Dagger fault
recovery. A portable scientific schema and finite oracles are better initial porting targets than
whole mature engines. Root license declarations, task names and source pins do not establish
complete artifact rights, installed-version compatibility or supported commercial equivalence.
[995, 996, 997, 998, 999, 1000, 1001, 1002, 1003]
Workflow retries need explicit logical identifiers, attempt histories and external-effect reconciliation.
A repeated USD100 instruction after lost acknowledgement can create USD200 of effects unless
the sink enforces an appropriate idempotency protocol. A constructed two-request example costs
eight units over four attempts; dividing only successful-attempt costs by completions reports one
unit rather than four. Neither figure is a measured service quote. The investment design must
count failures, retries, unresolved outcomes and complete resource and engineering costs before
claiming savings. [1004]
The next proposal milestone is a preregistered public suite with finite join and clock oracles,
uncertain-linkage cases, seeded logical replication, numeric cancellation, worker failures and sink
acknowledgement faults. CLI/MCP requests should bind immutable inputs, scientific target,
artifact profile and logical job identity, with typed results and diagnostic evidence. This comparison
creates no sibling-repository integration or native execution guarantee. Missing assignment or
availability truth cannot be recovered by a faster scheduler; it requires information or justified
restrictions. [1004]

and economically wrong. A second example assigns proxy scores 0.9 and 0.8 to actions worth
minus USD10 and plus USD20; optimizing the proxy createsUSD30 regret. These are finite design
witnesses, not observed bank losses or model error rates. [1007]
The public qualification plan separates translation, graph and identification correctness, valid
refusals, code/tool execution and decision utility. Correlated judges cannot be treated as indepen-
dent checks, passing sample tests cannot substitute for a specification, and agreement with noisy
benchmark labels cannot establish truth. For 100 requests, 80 completed runs and 60 accepted
scientific answers with USD200 total cost, the cost per accepted answer is USD10/3 rather than
USD2.50 per completion. This constructed accounting motivates complete denominators before
expensive bank trials. [1007]
Versioned CLI/MCP requests should retain the target, information clocks, assumptions, evidence,
worker scope and resource budget; results should bind artifacts, diagnostics, refusals and attempt
histories. This remains a proposal, not implemented repository integration. The review supplies a
route to public experiments without claiming universal causal identification, contamination-free
benchmarks, bank transfer or measured savings. [1007]


54.7    Qualify the scientific oracle before funding the agent trial

Public evidence can justify a prototype only if its reference preserves the intended decision. Inde-
pendent finite models demonstrate the instrumental-variable gap: identical observed distributions
and a ratio of 1 coexist with population effects of -1/2 and 1/4. A stipulated 10,000-account exam-
ple at USD100 per outcome unit implies increments of USD-500,000 and USD250,000 before costs.
This establishes ambiguity, not a bank estimate or measured benchmark failure. A prototype must
preserve ambiguity, change its target explicitly or identify the missing information. [1008, 1009]
Three reference states are required: identified, not identified and unknown. Backend failure
or nullable metadata cannot silently become a scientific label. Refusal, malformed output and
unknown truth have separate denominators. Interval validation covers endpoints, truth, confidence
level and overflow. The retained implementation reports raw and capped loss; an exact witness
shows why both matter, with raw means of 10 versus 1 changing to capped means of 0.1 versus 1.
[1008, 1009]
The public trial should bind native source and dependency versions, causal and scoring fixtures,
visible task information, and complete request, failure and cost counts. This work supplies
independently derived requirements and reproducible examples. Native conformance, generator
verification, artifact rights, independent review and banking value remain unqualified. The finite
findings justify a bounded experiment without establishing a completed product. [1009]


55     Measure the intervention before funding the language work-
       flow

A banking assistant that proposes rewriting a complaint response must distinguish the writer’s
intent, the customer’s perception, a classifier label and the actual editing procedure. The inspected
linguistic-effects preprint explicitly qualifies its directional attenuation argument by classifier

                                                 96
Causal banking: product proposal                                                     23 October 2026


quality and homogeneous effect direction. Its observational CFPB example uses outcome-matched
complaints and extreme politeness scores; it supplies a design reference, not known causal truth
for all bank customers. Public complaints and generated outcomes can justify a prototype before
an expensive bank experiment, provided their roles and selection limits remain explicit. [1010]
An independently authored, exactly enumerated example shows why this matters. Two equally
weighted populations have true effects of USD20 and minus USD10 per account. A proxy with
77.5 percent overall accuracy produces an adjusted contrast of minus USD4 although the true
average is plus USD5. For a stipulated 10,000 accounts, that is minus USD40,000 versus plus
USD50,000 before costs. The example uses heterogeneous effects and stochastic measurement;
it is neither a refutation of the source’s qualified result nor an observed TextCause failure. The
investment implication is to test conditional label errors and intervention meaning alongside
predictive accuracy. [1011]
Direct reuse has specific unresolved inputs. The pinned LLM-CD and LLM-and-SCD trees lack
root permission notices, while LCM’s code notice does not establish the identities, provenance and
terms of its externally linked pretrained models. Each needs component evidence and an admitted
environment before runtime adoption. A CLI or MCP response should expose unavailable artifacts
and execution failures explicitly. A separately reviewed public prototype can proceed where a
particular external artifact remains unavailable. These are precise adoption conditions, not reasons
to suspend independent proposal work. [1012]


56     Validate variable meaning before accepting the diagram

The inspected causal-modeling preprint evaluates whether phrases describe values of the same
variable and whether a phrase combines several supplied variables. Seven models generate and
evaluate the examples; the authors explicitly call for comparison with domain expertise. The tasks
are useful prototype designs, but the reported scores do not establish independently validated
banking variables or causal arrows. [1013]
The proposal separates semantic grouping, intervention definition and effect estimation. An exact
authored example shows that a phrase mentioning a payment holiday and outreach can have zero
additive causal interaction; a changed outcome law gives USD5 interaction with the same phrase.
Another finite example has a causal path but zero total mean effect because direct and mediated
changes cancel. The assistant must expose assumptions and ambiguity rather than turn phrase
matching or graph reachability into an effect guarantee. [1014]
A first public-data prototype should test incorrect variable merges and splits, time and unit
mismatches, unsupported certainty, deferred cases and review costs. The source’s anonymous code
link remains an unqualified adoption lead. A separately authorized implementation can proceed
with explicit variable records, source spans and unresolved alternatives before expensive bank
validation.


56.1    Separate causal reasoning from model repair

The selected Ma survey organizes discovery, effects, counterfactuals and explanations while warning
that collected evaluations use different prompts and task units. It supports a task-specific evaluation
contract, not a universal model ranking. The selected Causal-LLaVA preprint improves several
image-language scores, with some small regressions, and reports substantial training resources.
These are source-reported findings about perception, not evidence of bank-effect identification.
[1015, 1016]
An exact authored example separates arithmetic adjustment from normalized geometric approxi-


                                                  97
Causal banking: product proposal                                                 23 October 2026


mation and from changes in target weights. With a stipulated USD1,000 event loss, the resulting
expected losses are USD300,250,500 and740. These are mathematical examples,not observed
bank losses. The proposal retains Causal-LLaVA as a candidate measurement component,with
native artifact,rights,matched evaluation and bank transfer still open. CLI/MCP must keep the
measurement task,model intervention and business intervention distinct. [1017]


56.2    Preserve scientific meaning across provider responses

Pinned official OpenAI cookbook and Python SDK originals now complement the five existing
backend profiles. Strict schemas and parsing helpers constrain representation; refusals, incomplete
output and commentary require separate handling. The proposed adapter preserves these native
states and admits a scalar effect only after completion, representation checks and scientific
identification. A completed nonidentification answer remains a useful but distinct result. [1018,
1019]
An exact authored example gives six possible HTTP attempts when two workflow attempts each
allow three client attempts. At a stipulated USD 0.25 per attempt the cost is USD 1.50; the
stated timing path takes 33 seconds. These are illustrative accounting results, not vendor prices
or measured latency. Conditional adoption of the original client through a thin adapter avoids
an unjustified wholesale port, while model and service terms, native public fixtures and bank
effectiveness remain open. [1020]


56.3    Require scientific completion across framework boundaries

The selected framework delegates show why a completed workflow is insufficient evidence of a
valid causal answer. LangChain distinguishes schema representations and handled tool errors;
LlamaIndex can emit a terminal event after a structured-output warning. Semantic Kernel provides
provider/function hooks, while Haystack checks pipeline structure. The proposal conditionally
integrates these components behind a common scientific envelope that preserves nonidentification,
invalid output and incomplete execution. DSPy optimization requires separately held-out scientific
tasks and independent expected answers. [1021, 1022, 1023, 1024, 1025]
Exact source paths also motivate complete budgets and recovery records. Anthropic’s documented
eager tools can take effect before a reply finishes, and separate compaction calls need their own
usage accounting. Google client retry and timeout settings differ from workflow limits. Delegated
workflow serialization does not independently establish atomicity with remote effects. Native
stops, parser selection and schema-conversion diagnostics must survive vLLM and llama.cpp
compatibility interfaces; the inspected llama.cpp converter can warn and weaken an unsupported
pattern in a particular backend path. Ollama client identity remains separate from its server and
model. [1026, 1027, 1028, 1029, 1030, 1031]
A stipulated batch example makes five selective attempts versus nine whole-batch attempts,
costing USD 1.25 versus USD 2.25 at an illustrative USD 0.25 per attempt. A finite schema
example shows how weakening admits an extra value. These are exact authored witnesses, not
vendor prices or observed runtime failures. They define low-cost public fixtures before native
comparisons and expensive bank trials. Whole-repository assurance, hosted-service terms, exact
model artifacts, fresh visual review and final proposal acceptance remain open. [1032]

Six reviewed study designs give the prototype inexpensive routes forward. They are designs with
local checks, not newly executed or prospectively registered final studies. Before final evaluation,
each selected route must freeze its code and input identities, targets, splits or seeds, resource budget,
acceptance rule and failure accounting. Optional dataset access cannot block an independent
generated route. [66]

The suggested first empirical slice pairs formulation/timing cases with the generated randomized-
effect comparison. It is sufficiently narrow to compare complete workflows and has a known-truth
route without paid data. Add graph and linkage cases as deliberate refusal/ambiguity tests.
Servicing and dependent funding arguments can advance independently, but their unresolved
precision problems must not be hidden by a successful simpler demonstration.
For every offered task, record the scientific result, harmful confident errors, useful refusals, analyst
corrections, time and resource consumption. Compare the same information, target and failure
policy. Retain failed or unavailable requests in the denominator. A successful average can
otherwise conceal an unsafe or scientifically misleading subgroup. No elapsed-time improvement is
automatically a measured labor saving or a causal financial gain. [1033]


58     Economics and the next experiment

Let N be the number of attempted decision tasks in one year, D the incremental gross monetary
utility of the proposed action relative to the chosen baseline, F fixed incremental cost, c variable

                                                   99
Causal banking: product proposal                                                  23 October 2026


incremental cost per attempted task, and R an additional risk cost not already counted in D. A
transparent planning account is

                                   V = N E[D] − F − N c − R.

The baseline must be a feasible alternative under the same information. A correct refusal may
leave the baseline action unchanged while still costing review time. A misleading recommendation
can have negative utility. Loss must not be counted in both D and R, and unacceptable actions
remain excluded by constraints irrespective of their average financial payoff. [1033]


58.1    A reproducible illustration, with assumed inputs

The following one-year illustration uses USD solely as a declared planning unit. Assume 10,000
attempted tasks, fixed incremental cost of USD 100,000, variable incremental cost of USD 6 per
task and no separate R. All volumes, prices, gains, probabilities and signal likelihoods below
are assumptions; none is a measured bank benefit or vendor quote. The calculation excludes
discounting because its horizon is one year. [1033]


           Regime      Prior    Gross gain/task         Net value   Pr(positive signal)
           High           0.4          USD 40      USD 240,000                      0.8
           Moderate       0.4          USD 20       USD 40,000                      0.5
           Adverse        0.2          USD −2     USD −180, 000                     0.1


Expected incremental value is USD 76,000 before further information. This number alone cannot
justify a real investment because its inputs are assumed. Under the same assumptions, an
experiment produces a positive signal with probability 0.54. Expected net value conditional on
that signal is approximately USD 150,370, and conditional on a negative signal it is approximately
minus USD 11,304. Choosing to build only after a positive signal gives expected value USD 81,200
before the experiment’s own cost.
The expected value of sample information is consequently USD 5,200. An experiment costing USD
8,000 loses USD 2,800 in expected information value; at USD 3,000 it gains USD 2,200, assuming
identical signal informativeness. Perfect information has value USD 36,000 in this illustration.
That is an upper bound on the information component of spending for this particular decision
and prior, not a universal experiment budget. The prototype must help assess the assumed signal
quality before these calculations can inform an expensive study. [1033]
Holding other assumptions fixed, break-even gross gain is USD 16 per attempted task. Halving
volume raises it to USD 26; doubling volume reduces it to USD 11. An additional USD 4 of
review cost per task raises the 10,000-task threshold to USD 20. These sensitivities are arithmetic
consequences, not estimates of demand or analyst effort. Refusals and failed requests still incur
applicable costs and remain among the attempted tasks. [1034]


58.2    Costs to measure in the prototype

The measured account should separate data acquisition and cleaning, labels and identity audits,
model and tool calls, analyst formulation and checking, package setup, integration and maintenance,
compute and storage, incident recovery and independent validation. Each cost needs its unit,
quantity, date/version, price source and incremental share relative to the baseline. The current
actual-price fields remain unfilled. Public examples can reveal setup burden and local resource use
without establishing bank-scale service levels, customer outcomes or willingness to adopt. [1033]


                                               100
Causal banking: product proposal                                                 23 October 2026


A later bank experiment should state the decisions it distinguishes, the uncertain assumption that
matters, the target population and horizon, expected signals, losses from wrong decisions, and the
full cost of obtaining the information. It should compare cheaper public/generated alternatives
first. If no feasible experiment has plausible positive net information value, narrow or defer the
capability. A demand for authentic evidence becomes appropriate when that specific experiment
is justified; it is not a prerequisite for writing the current proposal.


59     What remains before a complete proposal

The cited Java snapshot had 55 open and 44 inconclusive software cells in its 102-cell matrix.
[448] The cited historical reuse register had 56 open and three inconclusive decisions among 115
candidates. Historical review counts are bound to the cited snapshot. [311] Historical review
counts are bound to the cited snapshot. [317] Historical review counts are bound to the cited
snapshot. [325] Historical review counts are bound to the cited snapshot. [330] Historical review
counts are bound to the cited snapshot. [335] The earlier totals are bound to their snapshot. [429]
The workflow-contract totals are bound to their snapshot. [436] These counts describe unresolved
review obligations, not numbers of defective products. Each closure needs an explicit original,
supported scope, comparison and adopt/integrate/port/reject decision. No absence finding follows
from an unread source. [277, 1035, 1036, 1037, 1038, 1039, 1040, 1041, 1042, 1043, 1044, 1045,
1046, 1047, 1048, 1049, 1050, 1051, 1052, 1053, 1054, 1055, 1056, 1057, 1058, 1059, 1060, 1061,
1062, 1063, 1064, 1065]
Twenty of 22 chapter reviews meet their current author criteria. L1 and L6 remain inconclusive
because frontier and framework coverage is incomplete despite meeting minimum depth. The
original-candidate census now contains 74 inspected and 39 unread originals out of 113; citation-
chain leads retain their own denominator. Fourteen task reviews and all twelve integration
requirements have scoped proposal outcomes, but those receipts do not establish a live model
benchmark or cross-repository adoption. The three mandatory repairs remain open. The agreed
depth contracts retain 100–150 substantive software pages, approximately 100 agent pages and
a developed integration treatment; repeated material does not earn duplicate credit. [277, 1066,
1067, 1068, 1] The historical collaboration census is bound to its release. [416] Current census
totals are bound to the unlearning release. [441]
The current sponsor update reconciles the recommendation, selected new evidence and remaining
work to this reader. Final proposal acceptance still requires complete coverage reconciliation
and rendered visual review of the exact release. Source audits, exact arithmetic and all-page
text bounds are narrower checks and cannot substitute for visual acceptance. Missing bank data,
platform access or signed adoption remain later conditional needs; they do not stop independent
proposal research. [2, 277]
The evidence routes are also different. We know how to complete missing source comparisons,
write and test selected adapters, run registered generated benchmarks and measure local workflow
costs. Their success still requires execution and review. We do not presently have a general useful
solution for arbitrary latent graphs, unrestricted persistent dependence, hidden-state optimal
policies or arbitrary nuisance learners. The proposal can be complete with explicit bounded claims
and negative findings in these areas; it cannot claim their scientific resolution. [2, 65, 68]
The present recommendation is therefore to test the narrow common service against existing
composition, use that evidence to refine the investment scenario, and request a bank experiment
only for a consequential question that the cheaper evidence cannot settle. Independent scientific
acceptance, target-bank validation, production reliability and adoption are later decisions with
their own evidence. They remain distinct from completing a credible proposal and a useful
bounded prototype.
